HKRBC Calculation Framework
香港風險為本資本制度 — 工作流筆記

Insurance (Valuation and Capital) Rules, Cap. 41R — Personal Study Notes
Effective Date: 1 July 2024  |  Basis: Cap. 41R · GL34 · IA Circulars (2024)  |  For reference only — not regulatory advice

§1 Framework Overview 框架總覽

HKRBC (Hong Kong Risk-Based Capital) came into force on 1 July 2024 alongside the Insurance (Amendment) Ordinance 2023. The framework uses an Economic Balance Sheet (EBS 經濟資產負債表) as its foundation. The Prescribed Capital Amount (PCA 訂明資本額) Cap. 41R Rule 37 is determined via stress-scenario methods across four risk modules, and compared against the available Capital Base (資本基礎) Cap. 41R Rule 7 to establish capital adequacy per Rule 5.

📌 Official Cap. 41R Terminology vs. Common Abbreviations
Common AbbreviationOfficial Cap. 41R Term (English)Source
PCA (or informally "PCR")prescribed capital amount 訂明資本額Rule 37; Part 5
current estimate (not "BEL")current estimate 現時估計值Rule 2, Rule 16
MOCEmargin over current estimate 現時估計邊際Rule 25
asset spread (≈ Z-Spread)asset spread 資產利差Sch. 5 §2
asset duration (≈ ESD)duration / asset dollar duration 資產價格存續期Sch. 5 §§2, 8
credit rating bandcredit rating band 信用評級等級Rule 2; Sch. 6

qualified LTAqualified LTA / qualified long term adjustment 具資格長期調整Rule 2; Sch. 5 §10
make-whole call optionmake-whole call option / make-whole call spread 全額提前贖回權Sch. 5 §4

§2 Economic Balance Sheet (EBS) 經濟資產負債表 Cap. 41R Part 2

Note: "Insurance liabilities" is the official Cap. 41R term (Rule 15). "Technical Provisions (TP)" is a Solvency II / Macao RBC term and does NOT appear in Cap. 41R. Similarly, "BEL (Best Estimate Liability)" is NOT a Cap. 41R term — the correct term is "current estimate" (Rule 16).

§3 Matching Adjustment (MA) 匹配調整 Cap. 41R Rule 24

MA is an additional spread applied to the risk-free discount rate for qualifying MA portfolios (官方 Cap. 41R 術語 Rule 2: "MA portfolio"). It allows a portion of the excess asset spread (above credit risk) to enhance the liability discount rate, thereby reducing the current estimate (現時估計值; 非 Cap. 41R 術語的 "BEL") and releasing capital.

Legal Basis 法律依據
Rule 24(1)Eligibility: MA portfolio must be physically or notionally segregated; assets and liabilities must be matched in cash flow terms.
Rule 24(2)MA = application ratio × adjusted spreadportfolio + constant prescribed spread component + qualified LTA (top-level formula per Sch. 5 §1).
Rule 24(6)Simplified approaches (a)–(g) available with IA approval for practical difficulties. IA Circular 12 Jun 2024, App. 2
Cap. 41R Sch. 5Detailed tables for risk correction (RC%), maximal make-whole call spreads, constant prescribed spreads (CPS), reduction factors (RF), and all sub-formula parameters.

Overall MA Formula 總公式 Rule 24(2)

$$MA = \underbrace{AR \times \text{adjusted spread}_{portfolio}}_{\text{Adjusted Spread Component}} + \underbrace{\text{constant prescribed spread component}}_{\text{CPS Component}} + \underbrace{\text{qualified LTA}}_{\text{LTA (if eligible per Rule 24(3))}}$$ $$\text{Discount Rate} = r_{RFR} + MA \quad \Rightarrow \quad \text{current estimate} = \sum_t \frac{CF_t}{(1 + r_{RFR} + MA)^t}\ \downarrow \quad \Rightarrow \quad NAV\ \uparrow$$ Formula per Cap. 41R Rule 24(2) & Sch. 5 §1(2). Note: "adjusted spread" and "application ratio" are the official Sch. 5 terms; "constant prescribed spread component" replaces informal "CPS Part".
flowchart LR subgraph ASSET["Asset Side 資產端"] direction TB ZS["Asset Spread per asset\n(Cap. 41R: 'asset spread', ≈Z-Spread)"] RC["Risk Correction RC%\n(by credit rating band, Sch. 6)"] ADD_B["Adjusted Spread per asset\n= asset spread × (1 − RC%)"] ADD_P["Portfolio Adjusted Spread\n(duration-weighted average, Sch. 5 §5)"] ZS & RC --> ADD_B --> ADD_P end subgraph LIAB["Liability Side 負債端"] direction TB ACS["Accumulated Cash Flow\nShortfall % (ACS%, Sch. 5 §7)"] PF["Predictability Factor (PF)\n= max(0, 1 − ACS%)"] ADD_L["Asset Dollar Duration\nvs Liability Dollar Duration (Sch. 5 §8)"] DF["Duration Factor (DF)\n= min(asset$/liab$, EA%)"] AR["Application Ratio (AR)\n= PF × DF (Sch. 5 §6)"] ACS --> PF ADD_L --> DF PF & DF --> AR end subgraph MA_BOX["MA Aggregation"] direction TB CPS_P["CPS Part\n= Wtd CPS × PF × gap%"] LTA["Qualified LTA\n(if applicable)"] MA_FINAL["MA = AR × ADD_portfolio\n+ CPS Part + LTA"] CPS_P & LTA --> MA_FINAL end ADD_P --> MA_BOX AR --> MA_BOX MA_FINAL --> DISC["Discount Rate = RFR + MA"] DISC --> BEL["BEL Calculation"] style MA_BOX fill:#e8f4f8,stroke:#2e7d9f

§3.1 Adjusted Spread (ADD) 調整後利差 Cap. 41R Sch. 5

Step 1: Asset Spread per bond 資產利差 Sch. 5 §§2, 5

Official Cap. 41R term: "asset spread" (資產利差) — defined in Schedule 5 §2 as "the constant spread that makes the price of an eligible asset equal to the present value of its cash flows when added to the specified risk-free yield curve at each point on the spot rate." In standard financial practice this is commonly called the Z-Spread. The value \(z\) is solved numerically:

$$Price_i = \sum_{t} \frac{CF_t}{(1 + r_t^{RFR} + z)^t} \quad \xrightarrow{\text{solve for}} \quad z = \text{asset spread}_i$$

Step 2: Asset Duration & Asset Dollar Duration 資產存續期 Sch. 5 §§2, 8

Official Cap. 41R term: "duration" (存續期) — defined in Schedule 5 §2 as "a measure of sensitivity of the value of asset or liability with respect to a change in interest rate … percentage change in price of the asset or value of the liability for a 100 basis point change in yield." In standard practice this corresponds to the ESD (Effective Spread Duration) for spread-sensitive assets.

$$\text{asset dollar duration} = \text{weighted average duration of eligible assets} \times MV_{\text{eligible assets}}$$ $$\text{liability dollar duration} = \text{effective duration of liabilities} \times CE_{\text{liabilities discounted at RFR}+MA_{est}}$$

Note: "asset dollar duration" and "liability dollar duration" are the official Schedule 5 §8 terms. These correspond to what is commonly called "dollar duration" or "DV01 × notional" in practice. Asset duration measures sensitivity to a 100 bp parallel shift in spread; it is used as the weighting metric in MA.

Step 3: Risk Correction & Adjusted Spread per asset Sch. 5 §5 Table 4 (RC%) & §4 Table 2 (Maximal Make-Whole Call Spread)

Risk correction removes the portion of asset spread attributable to expected credit losses. The official Cap. 41R formula from Schedule 5 §5(2): adjusted spread = Σ ωi × (spreadi − RCi), where RCi is the risk correction percentage.

$$\text{adjusted spread}_i = \text{asset spread}_i \times (1 - RC_i\%)$$

For assets with make-whole call options (official Cap. 41R term per Sch. 5 §4), the asset spread is capped at the maximal make-whole call spread before applying risk correction. Note: "MWC" used informally; official terms are "make-whole call option" and "make-whole call spread."

Credit Rating Band 信用評級等級 Cap. 41R Sch. 6Risk Correction (RC%) 風險修正比例 Sch. 5 §5 Table 4Maximal Make-Whole Call Spread (bps) Sch. 5 §4 Table 2
Band 1 (AAA / AA)11%30 bps
Band 2 (A)14%40 bps
Band 3 (BBB)20%60 bps
Band 4 or below (BB and below)22%80 bps
Sovereign (qualifying) 主權國債0%—

Credit rating bands 1–4 correspond to the mapping in Schedule 6 (Credit Rating Band) of Cap. 41R.

⚠️ Constraint adjusted spreadi ≥ 0 must always hold. Recognised Green Bonds: credit spread stress factor × 0.9 (10% discount).

Step 4: Portfolio-level weighted average adjusted spread Sch. 5 §§5, 9–11

The portfolio adjusted spread is the duration-weighted average across all eligible assets. The official weight ωi per Schedule 5 §5(2)(a) is based on the proportion of each asset group's market value multiplied by its asset duration:

$$\omega_i = \frac{MV_i \times \text{asset duration}_i}{\displaystyle\sum_j MV_j \times \text{asset duration}_j}$$ $$ADD_{portfolio} = \sum_i \omega_i \times ADD_i$$

§3.2 Application Ratio (AR) 應用比率 Cap. 41R Sch. 5 §§12–28

Predictability Factor (PF) 可預測性因子 Sch. 5 §§12–18

PF (predictability factor) measures how reliably asset cash flows can cover liability outflows even under extreme lapse scenarios. It is derived from the accumulated cash flow shortfall percentage (ACS%, official Cap. 41R term per Sch. 5 §7):

$$PF = \max\!\left(0,\ 1 - ACS\%_{max}\right)$$ $$ACS\%_{max} = \max\!\left(ACS\%_{Base},\ ACS\%_{Lapse\ Up},\ ACS\%_{Mass\ Lapse}\right)$$

ACS% for each scenario is computed by rolling forward a cumulative surplus/deficit account year by year:

$$Excess_t = Excess_{t-1} + Asset\_CF_t - Liab\_Net\_CF_t + Interest_{t-1}$$ $$Interest_{t-1} = \max(Excess_{t-1},\ 0) \times r_t^{RFR} \quad \text{(positive balance earns interest only)}$$ $$PV\_Shortfall_t = \begin{cases} |Excess_t| \times \displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{t}\frac{1}{1+r_s} & \text{if } Excess_t < 0 \\ 0 & \text{otherwise} \end{cases}$$ $$ACS\%_{scenario} = \frac{\displaystyle\max_{t=1}^{LLP}\ PV\_Shortfall_t}{MV_{Eligible\ Assets}}$$
Lapse Scenario 情景Liability Cash Flow Impact 負債端影響
BaseBest estimate lapse rates 最佳估計退保率
Lapse UpLapse rate × 1.40 (+40%); surrender benefit outflows increase
Mass Lapse30% of individual policies lapse in Year 1 (one-off shock)

Key constraints: Only cash flows within the last liquid point (LLP) Sch. 5 §7(6); Sch. 4 are considered. The last liquid point is the longest point at which the specified risk-free yield curve is liquid (per Sch. 5 §7(6) and Sch. 4). Liability outflows = guaranteed benefits only (future discretionary benefits / TVOG excluded); net of premium income. Asset cash flows are the same across all three scenarios (buy-and-hold strategy assumed).

Simplified Proxy for Lapse Scenarios Rule 24(6)(b) IA Circular 12 Jun 2024, App. 2(b)

$$ACS\%_{Lapse\ Up} = ACS\%_{Base} + 25\% \quad\quad ACS\%_{Mass\ Lapse} = ACS\%_{Base} + 25\%$$

Avoids running stressed actuarial models for lapse scenarios; subject to IA approval.

Duration Factor (DF) 久期因子 Sch. 5 §8

DF compares the asset dollar duration of eligible assets against the liability dollar duration of insurance liabilities subject to MA (official Cap. 41R terms per Sch. 5 §8):

$$\text{asset dollar duration} = \text{weighted average duration (eligible assets)} \times MV_{\text{eligible assets}}$$ $$\text{liability dollar duration} = \text{effective duration (liabilities)} \times CE_{\text{liabilities at RFR+MA}_{est}}$$ $$DF = \min\!\left(\text{eligible asset percentage},\ \frac{\text{asset dollar duration}}{\text{liability dollar duration}}\right); \quad \text{floored at 0}$$

Where eligible asset percentage (official Sch. 5 §8 term) = portion of eligible assets to total invested assets, capped at 100%. DF is floored at 0.

Application Ratio 應用比率 Sch. 5 §6

$$AR = PF \times DF \quad \text{(i.e. predictability factor × duration factor; both per Sch. 5 §6)}$$

Simplified Approach (a) Rule 24(6)(a): AR = 15% flat at entity level, no constant prescribed spread component recognised.

Constant Prescribed Spread Component 固定訂明利差組成部分 Cap. 41R Sch. 5 §9 Table 5 (CPS rates)

The constant prescribed spread component (official Cap. 41R term per Sch. 5 §9; informally abbreviated "CPS") compensates for the "duration gap" — the portion of eligible assets whose duration cannot be matched to liabilities. It applies to all MA portfolios (Par, Non-Par, Capital) for investment-grade eligible assets only. Sovereign bonds of qualifying jurisdictions: constant prescribed spread = 0 (per Sch. 5 §9(3)).

$$CPS\ Part = Weighted\ CPS_{portfolio} \times PF \times \max\!\left[\min\!\left(20\%,\ EA\% - \frac{ADD}{LDD}\right),\ 0\right]$$

where \(EA\%\) = eligible assets as a proportion of total invested assets; the gap \(EA\% - ADD/LDD\) is capped at 20% and floored at 0.

If \(ADD_{portfolio} < Weighted\ CPS\), substitute \(ADD_{portfolio}\) for \(Weighted\ CPS\).

Maturity Bucket 期限組別 Sch. 5 §9 Table 5Rating 1 (AAA/AA)Rating 2 (A)Rating 3 (BBB)Rating 4+ (BB–)
0 – 5 yrs20 bps30 bps45 bps60 bps
5 – 10 yrs35 bps45 bps60 bps70 bps
10 – 15 yrs40 bps50 bps65 bps80 bps
15 – 20 yrs50 bps60 bps75 bps90 bps
20 – 25 yrs50 bps60 bps75 bps90 bps
25 – 30 yrs50 bps60 bps75 bps90 bps
30+ yrs50 bps60 bps75 bps90 bps

Note: If \(ADD_{portfolio} < Weighted\ CPS\), substitute \(ADD_{portfolio}\) for \(Weighted\ CPS\) per Sch. 5 §9(4). Sovereign bonds (qualifying): CPS = 0 per Sch. 5 §9(3).

Qualified LTA 合格長期調整 Cap. 41R Sch. 5 §§29–35

Additional adjustment for qualifying portfolios (physically segregated participating or universal life business) to reflect the liquidity premium of long-term equity and property holdings:

$$Qualified\ LTA = LTA_{prescribed} \times \min\!\left(\frac{MV_{Equity+Property}}{MV_{Total\ Invested\ Assets}},\ Cap_{30y+}\right)$$ $$Cap_{30y+} = \frac{\displaystyle\sum_{t>30} PV(Liability\ CF_t)}{\displaystyle\sum_{all\ t} PV(Liability\ CF_t)}$$

\(LTA_{prescribed} = 100\ bps\). The equity/property proportion is capped by the weight of liabilities with duration > 30 years, preventing over-recognition.

Note 備註 Qualified LTA is typically set to zero if the portfolio does not meet the physical segregation requirement.

§3.3 Simplified Approaches 簡化方法 (Cap. 41R Rule 24(6); IA Circular 12 Jun 2024, Appendix 2)

Simplified Approach 簡化方法Description & Formula 說明與公式Applicability 適用條件
a) Proxy AR = 15% \(AR = 15\%\) for all portfolios; no CPS Part recognised Practical difficulty calculating portfolio AR
b) ACS% additive proxy +25% \(ACS\%_{Lapse\ Up} = ACS\%_{Mass\ Lapse} = ACS\%_{Base} + 25\%\) Stressed actuarial runs not available
c) Prescribed haircuts on stressed ESD Apply prescribed reductions to Base \(ADD\) / \(LDD\) for IR Down and Equity scenarios Stressed ESD cannot be recalculated
d/e) Data recency (±1 month) Policy / market data within 1 month of valuation date allowed; must evidence no material difference Data unavailable at exact valuation date
f) Interpolation of TVOG Reference points ≤ 50 bps apart; interpolation permitted, extrapolation not Reduces stochastic simulation runs
g) Round MA down to nearest 10 bps Final MA is rounded down to the nearest 10 bps in each scenario (base and all PCR stress scenarios) 最終MA向下取整至最接近10bps Reduces precision requirements in liability system
Consistency Requirement 一致性要求 Rule 24(6), IA Circular 12 Jun 2024, App. 2
Once a simplified approach is elected, it must be applied consistently across:
  • All MA portfolios of the insurer (same approach entity-wide);
  • All scenarios — base case, IR Up, IR Down, Credit Spread, and Equity PCR calculations.
The insurer must be able to demonstrate prudence over the default calculation or demonstrate no major solvency impact, and provide evidence to the IA on request.

§3.4 Stressed MA 壓力情景下的匹配調整 Cap. 41R Sch. 5 §§36–46

Default Approach 默認法

Recalculate stressed ADD and stressed LDD under each scenario; AR is updated accordingly.

Stress ScenarioADD ChangeAR / DF ChangeApproach
IR UpLargely unchangedLiability ESD shortens → DF may fallDefault
IR DownLargely unchangedLiability ESD lengthens → DF may rise; or use haircutDefault / Simplified (c)
Credit Spread (CS) — Dynamic MASee Dynamic MA belowAR held at Base; ADD increases then reduced by RFDynamic MA
EquityLargely unchangedUse Base AR or apply haircut to ESDDefault / Simplified (c)

Dynamic MA for Credit Spread Stress 信用利差壓力下的動態匹配調整 Sch. 5 Table 4 (RF)

When credit spreads widen, Z-Spreads increase, causing ADD to rise — but this increase must be partially offset by a Reduction Factor (RF) (a regulatory haircut) to avoid over-recognition of stressed spread in the discount rate.

$$MA_{CS\ stressed} = AR \times \left[ADD_{base} + RF \times \left(ADD_{stressed} - ADD_{base}\right)\right] + CPS\ Part_{stressed}$$
MA Rating BandReduction Factor (RF) 削減因子 Sch. 5 Table 4
Rating 10.35
Rating 20.40
Rating 30.50
Rating 40.60
Rating 50.70

A higher RF means more of the stressed spread increase is recognised in MA, partially offsetting the liability impact of CS stress. Lower-rated assets get a higher RF because their spread widening is more likely to reflect genuine liquidity rather than credit deterioration.

§4 Prescribed Capital Amount (PCA) 訂明資本額 Cap. 41R Part 5 (Rules 37–86)

📌 Official Terminology Note 官方術語注意： The official Cap. 41R term is "prescribed capital amount" (訂明資本額), abbreviated as PCA. The abbreviation "PCR" (Prescribed Capital Requirement) is widely used by practitioners but does not appear in Cap. 41R. See Rule 37(1) and Part 5 title. Similarly, each module produces a "risk capital amount" (not "PCR for risk"), per Rule 37(1)(a)–(b).
\[ \mathrm{PCA} = \mathrm{RCA}_{\text{aggregate}} - \mathrm{LAC(FDB)} - \mathrm{LAC(DT)} + \mathrm{RCA}_{\text{op risk}} \] \[ \mathrm{RCA}_{\text{aggregate}} = \sqrt{\sum_{x,y} \rho_{xy} \cdot \mathrm{RCA}_x \cdot \mathrm{RCA}_y} \]
RCA = risk capital amount 風險資本額  |  LAC = loss absorbing capacity 損失吸收能力  |  FDB = future discretionary benefits 未來酌情利益  Rule 37(1)(c) & Sch. 7 Table 2

§4.1 Market Risk 市場風險 Cap. 41R Rules 45–51; Rule 46 for MA recalculation

Sub-riskAsset Shock 資產端衝擊Liability Shock 負債端衝擊Representative Stress Factor
IR Up 利率上升 Rule 47✅ Bonds repriced at stressed discount rate✅ BEL recalculated (higher rate → lower BEL); MA recalculated per Rule 46 & Sch. 5 §8Curve shifts up (prescribed per Rule 47 & Sch. 7 Table 3A)
IR Down 利率下降 Rule 47✅ Bonds repriced✅ BEL increases; MA recalculated per Rule 46 & Sch. 5 §8Curve shifts down (prescribed per Rule 47 & Sch. 7 Table 3B)
Credit Spread (CS) 信用利差 Rule 48✅ Bonds repriced (Z-Spread + CS stress)✅ BEL recalculated + Dynamic MA (ADD & DF recalculated per Rule 46, Sch. 5 §§5, 8)Rating × maturity factor matrix (Rule 48 & Sch. 7)
Equity 權益 Rule 49✅ Equity MV × (1 + stress factor + CCA)❌ BEL holds at Base; DF may use haircut per Rule 46 & Sch. 5 §8By asset class (Rule 49); CCA per Rule 49(2)(a)
Property 物業 Rule 50✅ Property MV × (1 − 25%)❌ No change−25%
Currency 貨幣 (FX) Rule 51✅ Net exposure per currency × FX stress factor (HKD vs MOP = 0%)Per Rule 51 currency factor table

§4.1.1 Equity Countercyclical Adjustment (CCA) 權益順周期調整

⚠️ HKRBC-specific mechanism (IA Circular, 21 Jun 2024) Per Cap. 41R Rule 49(2)(a), the adjusted equity downward stress factor = Table 5 prescribed factor + CCA (CCA per Rule 49(2)(a)).
During the Interim Period (until IA concludes its CCA review study), each insurer must notify IA of one of the following (irrevocable election, by 15 Jul 2024):
  (a) Adopt the CCA published by IA (based on CCA Generation Tool specifications, 9 July 2021); or
  (b) Set CCA = 0 (no countercyclical adjustment applied).

§4.2 Life Insurance Risk 人壽保險風險 Cap. 41R Rules 52–59; HRG definition: Rule 53

Common Mechanism 共同機制 All life insurance risk scenarios shock the liability side only (BEL is recalculated; assets remain at Base). Rule 52
PCRsub-risk = ΣHRG max( ΔBELhrg + ΔTVOGhrg, 0 ) — aggregated using Sch. 7 Table 4 (life sub-risk correlation matrix)
Sub-risk 子風險Stress Applied 壓力Affected Products 受影響產品
Mortality Risk 死亡率風險Permanent multiplicative +12.5% on mortality rate (×1.125)Life protection products (death benefit)
Longevity Risk 長壽風險Permanent multiplicative −17.5% on mortality rate (×0.825)Annuities / retirement protection
Life CAT Risk 人壽巨災風險Year 1 additive +1.5‰ on mortality rate (short-term shock)Life protection products
Morbidity Risk 疾病風險 (4 categories) Cat 1 Medical: +20% / +8%; Cat 2 Lump-sum CI: +25% / +20%;
Cat 3 Short Periodic: +20% / +12%; Cat 4 Long Periodic: +25% / +20% (recovery rate −20%)		 By product / benefit category
Expense Risk 費用風險
4 geographic regions — HKRBC-specific		 Applied simultaneously 同時施加:
① Multiplicative +8% on expense level (all years 永久);
② Additive increase on expense inflation 費用通脹:
   HK: Yr 1–10 +3%, Yr 11–20 +2%, Yr 21+ +1%
   (Non-HK regions have separate prescribed rates per Rule 62) 		All HRGs
Lapse Up 退保上升Lapse rate ×1.40 (+40%), capped at 100%Products where higher lapse is adverse
Lapse Down 退保下降Lapse rate ×0.60 (−40%)Products where lower lapse is adverse
Mass Lapse 大規模退保Individual 30%; Group 20% (one-off mass surrender)Individual / Group policies

Lapse PCR 退保風險資本要求 = max( Level/Trend component, Mass Lapse component ) Rule 59

§4.3 General Insurance (GI) Risk 非壽險風險 Cap. 41R Rules 60–80

Sub-riskMethodNotes
Reserve Risk 準備金風險 Rule 64Factor × Net outstanding claims reserve 淨未付賠款準備金Factor per lines of business (Sch. 9); aggregated with Premium Risk per Rule 63 & Sch. 7 Table 7
Premium Risk 保費風險 Rule 65Factor × Expected net earned premium (next 12 months)Factor per lines of business (Sch. 9); Reserve & Premium combined per Rule 63, Sch. 7 Table 7 (ρ=50%)
Natural Catastrophe Risk 自然災害風險 Rule 67–68 Factor-based (Rule 68) or Own Assessment (OA) with IA approval (Rule 67) OA application: IA Circular 12 Jun 2024, Appendix 3;
Man-made non-systematic CAT: Rule 69; Man-made systematic CAT: Rule 70
Mortgage Insurance Risk 按揭保險風險 Rules 71–80 Separate factor-based treatment; onshore standard/reverse mortgage and offshore classes each have distinct rules GI sub-risks aggregated per Sch. 7 Table 5 (GI overall) and Table 6 (Reserve & Premium vs CAT: ρ=25%)

§4.4 Counterparty Default & Other Risk 交易對手違責及其他風險 Cap. 41R Rules 81–85

Exposures subject to counterparty default risk include: reinsurance recoverables (RI BEL), reinsurance receivables, outstanding premiums, deposits with banks, derivatives (OTC), and other balance sheet assets not covered by market/life/GI risk modules.

\[ \mathrm{RCA}_{\text{Counterparty}} = \sum_{i} \mathrm{Exposure}_i \times \mathrm{RiskFactor}_i \]

Unrated — all others38.8% (= Rating 7)Rule 81: most conservative treatment; Cap. 41R Sch. 6 for rating band mapping

§4.5 Operational Risk 業務操作風險 Cap. 41R Rule 86

Life Non-Linked Business 非聯結人壽業務

\[ \mathrm{OP}_{\text{Total}} = \mathrm{OP}_{\text{Prem}} + \mathrm{OP}_{\text{PremGrowth}} + \mathrm{OP}_{\text{Liab}} \] \[ \mathrm{OP}_{\text{Prem}} = 4\% \times P_t \qquad \text{(gross written premium)} \] \[ \mathrm{OP}_{\text{PremGrowth}} = 4\% \times \max(0,\; P_t - 1.2 \times P_{t-1}) \qquad \text{(growth beyond 20\% threshold)} \] \[ \mathrm{OP}_{\text{Liab}} = 0.45\% \times \frac{CE_t + CE_{t-1} + CE_{t-2}}{3} \qquad \text{(3-year avg current estimate)} \]

Life Linked Business 聯結人壽業務: No premium factor; liability factor = 0.40% of 3-year average current estimate.
Class H (Pension) 養老業務: Follows Life Linked treatment for QIS 2.

\[ \mathrm{RCA}_{\text{OP}} = \max\!\Bigl(\text{Floor},\; \min\!\bigl(\text{Cap},\; \mathrm{OP}_{\text{Total}}\bigr)\Bigr) \] \[ \text{Cap} = \mathrm{RCA}_{\text{pre-OP}} \times 30\% \qquad \text{Floor} = \text{HKD } 15{,}000\text{k (life)} \]

PCROP is added after risk aggregation: PCRfinal = PCRaggregate − LAC + PCROP

§4.6 Loss Absorbing Capacity (LAC) 損失吸收能力 Cap. 41R Rules 43–44

Type 類型Description 說明Rule Reference
LAC of Future Discretionary Benefits 未來酌情利益損失吸收能力
Official Rule 43 title: "Adjustment to prescribed capital amount to reflect the loss absorbing capacity of future discretionary benefits." Often informally called "LAC(FDB)" or "LAC-TP".		 The additional reduction in future discretionary benefits (FDB) achievable under stress through extra management actions (e.g., reducing bonuses/dividends in adverse scenarios). Capped at max(PV FDBbase − PV FDBminimum, 0). Excess above cap added back to PCA per Rule 37(1)(d). Rule 43
LAC of Deferred Tax 遞延稅項損失吸收能力
Official Rule 44 title: "Adjustment to prescribed capital amount to reflect loss absorbing capacity of deferred tax." Often informally called "LAC(DT)".		 Tax savings arising from a stressed loss position (effective tax rate, capped at 16.5% per Rule 44(2)(b)). Must be demonstrably realisable — requires evidence of future taxable income. No mechanical recognition allowed. Rule 44

§5 Participating Fund Management (GL34) 參與型保單基金管理 GL34 (IA Guideline, effective 1 Jul 2024)

GL34 (Guideline on Establishment and Maintenance of Funds in respect of Participating Business) took effect on 1 July 2024, setting out IA's expectations for governance of participating funds (參與型基金) maintained under section 21B of the Insurance Ordinance (Cap. 41). Physical segregation of assets under GL34 is also a prerequisite for MA portfolio eligibility under Cap. 41R Rule 24(1).

GL34 SectionKey Requirement 主要要求
§3 Asset & Liability Identification 資產負債識別 Clearly identify assets and liabilities attributable to participating business; riders/FOD/prepaid premiums assessed separately based on substance over form
§4 Opening Balance 期初餘額 On effective date, assets in participating fund must not be less than the amount attributable to participating business; changes of basis or one-off distributions since Jan 2019 subject to independent review
§5 Expenses & Charges 費用分配 Allocations must be fair, equitable and reasonable; costs must benefit policyholders; related-party transactions at arm's length; inflated charges treated as profit distribution
§6 Distributable Surplus / Profits 盈餘分配 Profit-sharing mechanism must be documented in Board-approved corporate policy; policyholder fair treatment must be demonstrable; IA may require independent assessment
§7 Capital Support 資本支持 If participating fund assets are insufficient to cover liabilities, additional assets must be transferred promptly
§8 Physical Segregation 資產隔離 Insurer must maintain books to identify assets attributable to each participating fund; physical segregation may not be legally required but records must be traceable
§9 Independent Report 獨立報告 On effective date, an external independent person must assess whether the participating fund has been properly established in compliance with GL34

§6 Capital Requirements & Capital Base 資本要求與資本基礎 Cap. 41R Part 2 (Rule 5) & Part 3 (Rules 7–10)

Tier 層級Composition 構成Limit 限額 Rule 7(b)–(c)
Unlimited Tier 1 capital (UT1) 無限制一級資本 Ordinary shares, retained earnings, other qualifying UT1 instruments Sch. 1; less deductions (intangibles, net DTA, crypto assets, etc.) per Rule 8(3) No limit — forms the core Rule 8
Limited Tier 1 capital (LT1) 有限制一級資本 Hybrid instruments meeting Sch. 2 criteria (permanence, loss-absorption, subordination)
Official term: "Limited Tier 1 capital instrument" per Rule 2		 ≤ 10% of PCA Rule 7(b)
Tier 2 capital 二級資本 Subordinated debt and other instruments meeting Sch. 3 criteria
Official term: "Tier 2 capital instrument" per Rule 2		 ≤ 50% of PCA Rule 7(c)
Capital Requirement (Rule 5): Capital Base ≥ max( PCA, 50% × PCA, HKD 20,000,000 )   Rule 5(1)(a)–(c)
Capital Base = UT1 + LT1 + Tier 2 capital (subject to limits per Rule 7)   Rule 7(a)
PCA = prescribed capital amount 訂明資本額 (Rule 37); commonly called "PCR" by practitioners.

§7 HKRBC vs Macao RBC — Key Differences 主要差異對照

Dimension 維度HKRBC (Cap. 41R)Macao RBC (MRBC)
Effective Date 生效日期 1 July 2024 QIS 2 (testing phase, 2025)
Expense Risk Regions 費用風險地區分組 4 geographic regions (HK / Non-HK Developed / Non-HK Emerging / Other) — each with distinct inflation stress parameters Single unified parameter set (all regions)
Equity CCA 順周期調整 Rule 49(2)(a); during Interim Period, insurer may elect CCA = 0 or use IA CCA Generation Tool value (irrevocable) CCA exists under MRBC Appendix VI; specific parameters TBC
MA Simplified Approaches 匹配調整簡化方法 Rule 24(6): 6 prescribed simplifications, including 15% proxy AR and +25% additive ACS% proxy (IA Circular Appendix 2) MA Tech Spec §59: simplified haircut approaches; slightly different parameters and structure
Participating Fund Regulation 參與型基金監管 GL34 (detailed): covers surplus distribution, expense allocation, physical segregation, and independent report requirements No equivalent dedicated guideline under MRBC QIS 2
Natural CAT Own Assessment 自然巨災自評法 Available under Rule 67 — requires IA approval; detailed application and ongoing reporting process (IA Circular Appendix 3) Not applicable (GI exposure minimal)
ESG Minimum Requirements Codified in IA Circular (12 Jun 2024, Appendix 1): ≥ 1,000 scenarios, martingale test, implied volatility test, annual re-calibration MA Tech Spec: similar stochastic simulation requirements; parameters broadly consistent
Unrated Counterparty Factor Regulated RBC entity: 6.2% (avg Rating 4+5); Others: 38.8% (= Rating 7) Same rule (Main Tech Spec §615)
MOCE Treatment Included in TP; not deducted from PCR; allocated to participating funds separately (per CoC 4%) Same treatment (Main Tech Spec)

§8 Economic Scenario Generator (ESG) 經濟情景模擬器

§8.0 香港保險行業 ESG 實操概覽

香港保險公司極少自建 ESG 引擎，而是採購商業化 ESG 平台，配合 IA 發布的監管數據進行校準。以下是行業的典型做法。

1. 主流商業 ESG 軟件

軟件供應商HK 市場使用情況主要特點
Moody's Analytics ESG
（前 RiskIntegrity / AXIS ESG）		 Moody's Analytics 大型壽險公司最常用，尤其是有母公司使用 Moody's AXIS 的 支持 HW1F/HW2F/LMM；內建 martingale test；可導出為 Prophet 格式
Conning GEMS
（Global Economic Model Scenarios）		 Conning 以 Real-World ALM 分析為主的公司常用 Real-World 和 Risk-Neutral 雙模式；P-measure 宏觀校準功能強
Milliman GEMS / MG-ALFA ESG Milliman 使用 MG-ALFA 建模平台的公司 與 MG-ALFA 原生整合，適合 US/HK GAAP 混合項目
FIS Prophet 內建 ESG FIS（現 SS&C） 使用 Prophet 進行 TVOG 計算的公司；在 HK 壽險市場佔有率高 直接在 Prophet 模型中調用情景文件（.ESC 格式）；支持 HW1F 標準模型；校準需外部工具輔助
自建 Python/MATLAB 少數大型集團 母公司集中校準，子公司使用統一情景庫 靈活但需投入大量開發和驗證資源

2. 典型 ESG 生產流程（以 TVOG 為目標）

步驟操作數據來源 / 工具頻率
① 取得 IA 監管數據 下載 IA 發布的 Schedule 4 無風險利率曲線（Smith-Wilson 外插後的零息曲線）；確認 UFR（見 IA 憲報通告） IA 官網 → HKRBC 資源頁；G.N. 4006 of 2024 等 每季（或監管要求時更新）
② 取得市場波動率數據 從 Bloomberg 提取 swaption vol cube（USSV/HDSV）、equity vol surface（OVDV）、credit spread 曲線（CDSW） Bloomberg Terminal；路透社 Eikon 每季末（估值日）
③ 校準 ESG 參數 將 HW2F 的 (a₁, a₂, σ₁, σ₂, ρ) 或 LMM 的 vol 參數校準至 swaption vol cube；目標：最小化模型 IV 與市場 IV 的差距 ESG 軟件內建優化器；或 Python scipy.optimize 每季
④ 生成情景 以 Q-measure 參數生成 ≥1,000 條 monthly 情景（TVOG 用）；另生成 Real-World 情景（ALM 用） ESG 軟件；輸出格式：CSV / Prophet .ESC 文件 每季
⑤ Martingale Test 驗證：所有情景下折現資產價格的均值 = 今日市場價格（零息債券、股票指數）；不通過則重新調整參數 ESG 軟件內建；或 Python 腳本 每次生成後必做
⑥ 導入精算模型 將情景文件導入 Prophet（.ESC 格式）或其他模型；Prophet 按情景逐條計算 TVOG；取平均值即 TVOG Prophet ActPlan / AXIS / MG-ALFA 每季估值
⑦ 情景驗證 & 記錄 比較情景統計特性（均值、百分位、波動率）與市場預期是否一致；出具模型驗證報告供 actuarial function 審核及 IA 查閱 內部 Model Validation Team；外部顧問 每年至少一次完整驗證

3. 監管要求摘要（Cap. 41R）

要求條款行業實操
情景數量Rule 19：≥1,000 條常用 1,000–5,000 條；更多情景提高收斂精度但增加運算時間
市場一致性Rule 19：market-consistent以 Q-measure 校準；swaption vol 為主要校準目標
無套利Rule 19：arbitrage-freeMartingale test 必須通過
無風險折現率Schedule 4IA 每季發布；Smith-Wilson 外插至 60 年
UFRIA Gazette Notice（G.N. 4006/2024）HKD/USD 3.8%；RMB 4.6%；EUR 3.45%；定期由 IA 更新

4. Prophet 與 ESG 的接口（HK 最常見組合）

Prophet 讀取的情景文件為 .ESC 格式（ASCII 文本），每行一個時間步的情景值。典型結構： 

SCENARIO_COUNT  1000
TIME_STEPS      360        ! 30年 × 12月
VARIABLES       RFR_SHORT  EQUITY_TR  CREDIT_SPREAD  FX_USD_HKD

SCENARIO  1
  0.0320  1.0000  0.0085  7.8000
  0.0318  1.0065  0.0087  7.8012
  ...
SCENARIO  2
  0.0315  0.9980  0.0092  7.7998
  ...

Prophet 內的 Global Parameter ESG_FILE_PATH 指向此文件；模型按 SIM_NUM（情景編號）逐條執行，輸出各情景的 BEL；TVOG = 隨機 BEL 均值 − 確定性 BEL。

5. 常見實操挑戰

挑戰說明業界應對
運算時間1,000 情景 × 大型模型 → 數小時至數天模型點壓縮（MP aggregation）；AWS/Azure 雲端並行計算；Prophet 的 Grid Computing
Martingale test 不收斂HW 參數校準後仍有小幅 martingale 誤差允許 ±0.5% 容差（視公司 policy）；或增加情景數至 2,000–5,000
HK Swaption 流動性HKD swaption OTM 流動性差，vol 數據不可靠以 USD swaption vol 為主，HKD 用 basis 調整；或直接用 IA Sch. 4 forward rates 倒推隱含 vol
UFR 更新滯後IA 每幾年更新一次 UFR，短期內 yield curve 外插段出現跳躍建立 UFR 敏感度分析；每次估值確認最新 Gazette Notice
跨貨幣一致性HKD/USD/RMB 多幣種資產組合需確保 FX 路徑無套利加入 covered interest parity 約束；Cholesky 確保跨幣種相關性一致
商業軟件授權費用高 Moody's / Conning GEMS 年費 USD 150k–500k+，中小型公司難以負擔 可使用開源 Python ESG 替代——見下方開源項目 ↓

🔗 開源替代方案：hkrbc-esg（Python）

針對 Par Fund TVOG 開發的輕量級 Python ESG 生成器，符合 Cap. 41R Rule 19 要求，適合不想採購商業軟件的中小型香港保險公司使用。

功能說明
利率模型Hull-White 1-Factor（Q-measure，精確離散化），自動擬合 IA Schedule 4 yield curve
股票模型GBM（Q-measure），drift = r(t)，確保無套利
Martingale Test自動驗證；對偶變量法（antithetic variates）降低誤差至 ≤1%（1–15Y）
輸出格式CSV（通用）+ Prophet .ESC（FIS Prophet 直接讀取）
配置方式YAML 文件，5 分鐘更新 IA Schedule 4 利率曲線
費用免費，MIT License
適用場景Par Fund TVOG、IFRS 17 MRB、ALM Real-World 情景

快速開始：

pip install -r requirements.txt
python examples/run_esg.py          # 生成 1,000 條情景，自動通過 Martingale Test
# → output/esg_scenarios.csv       導入任意精算平台
# → output/esg_scenarios.ESC       FIS Prophet 直接讀取

📂 GitHub 倉庫： github.com/woming98/hkrbc-esg  |  文檔：見倉庫 README.md

用途情景類型 ※情景數量說明
HKRBC TVOG / EBS 估值Risk-neutral（風險中性）≥1,000 條 Rule 19martingale test 校準；僅用於計算合約期權/保證的時間價值，不用於 PCR 壓力測試
ALM 壓力測試 / SAA / ORSAReal-world（真實世界）100–500 條歷史回歸＋宏觀假設校準；測試資產負債缺口
對沖策略校準Real-world視需要hedge ratio 優化、IRS / CDS 效果評估

※ Risk-neutral vs Real-world 的區別：見本節末尾說明 ↓

同一引擎、兩套參數集：風險中性參數由 martingale test 校準；真實世界參數由歷史回歸＋宏觀假設確定。

Risk Factor Coverage 風險因子覆蓋

以下六類風險因子各自有對應的隨機模型。每個因子卡片列出：可選模型（含 SDE 和參數說明）、校準目標、Python 實現方式及 HK 市場適用建議。

IR Nominal Interest Rate  名義利率 校準目標：IA Sch.4 yield curve + swaption vol surface

① HW1F 起步首選 · 2 參數

\[ dr(t) = \bigl[\theta(t) - a\,r(t)\bigr]\,dt + \sigma\,dW^{\mathbb{Q}}(t) \]

\(a\)：均值回歸速度 | \(\sigma\)：波動率 | \(\theta(t)\) 由 Sch.4 曲線唯一確定

Yield curve 拟合 — α(t)

\[ \alpha(t) = f(0,t) + \frac{\sigma^2}{2a^2}(1-e^{-at})^2 \]

確保 \(\mathbb{E}^{\mathbb{Q}}[r(t)] = f(0,t)\)（精確擬合初始 yield curve）

零息債券解析公式

\[ P(t,T) = A(t,T)\,e^{-B(t,T)\,r(t)}, \quad B = \tfrac{1-e^{-a\tau}}{a} \] \[ \ln A = \ln\tfrac{P(0,T)}{P(0,t)} + B\,f(0,t) - \tfrac{\sigma^2}{4a}(1-e^{-2at})B^2 \]

條件分布（正態）· Martingale Test

\[ r(t)\,\big|\,r(0)\;\sim\;\mathcal{N}\!\bigl(\alpha(t),\;\tfrac{\sigma^2}{2a}(1-e^{-2at})\bigr) \] \[ \mathbb{E}^{\mathbb{Q}}\!\left[e^{-\int_0^T r\,ds}\right] = P(0,T) \;\checkmark \]

▶ hkrbc-esg src/hw1f.py · --ir-model HW1F

② HW2F / G2++ 主流生產 · 5 參數

\[ r(t) = x(t)+y(t)+\varphi(t) \] \[ dx = -a\,x\,dt+\sigma_1 dW_1,\quad dy = -b\,y\,dt+\sigma_2 dW_2 \] \[ dW_1 dW_2 = \rho_{xy}\,dt \]

\(a,b\)：回歸速度（慢/快因子）| \(\sigma_1,\sigma_2\)：各因子 vol | \(\rho_{xy}\)：因子相關（典型 −0.8）

φ(t) — Yield curve 精確擬合

\[ \varphi(t) = f(0,t) + \frac{\sigma_1^2}{2a^2}(1-e^{-at})^2 + \frac{\sigma_2^2}{2b^2}(1-e^{-bt})^2 + \frac{\rho_{xy}\sigma_1\sigma_2}{ab}(1-e^{-at})(1-e^{-bt}) \]

零息債券解析公式（G2++ 閉合解）

\[ P(t,T) = A(t,T)\exp\!\bigl(-B_a(\tau)x - B_b(\tau)y\bigr) \] \[ B_a(\tau)=\tfrac{1-e^{-a\tau}}{a},\quad B_b(\tau)=\tfrac{1-e^{-b\tau}}{b} \] \[\ln A = \ln\tfrac{P(0,T)}{P(0,t)}+B_a f(0,t)+B_b f(0,t) - \tfrac{1}{2}V(t,T) \]

\(V(t,T)\) 為 \(x+y\) 的條件方差（含交叉項 \(\rho_{xy}\sigma_1\sigma_2\)）

精確離散化（避免 Euler 偏差）

\[ x_{t+\Delta} = x_t e^{-a\Delta} + \sigma_1\sqrt{\tfrac{1-e^{-2a\Delta}}{2a}}\,Z_1 \] \[ y_{t+\Delta} = y_t e^{-b\Delta} + \sigma_2\sqrt{\tfrac{1-e^{-2b\Delta}}{2b}}\,Z_2,\quad Z_1 Z_2=\rho_{xy} \]

▶ hkrbc-esg src/hw2f.py · --ir-model HW2F · Martingale ✅

③ LMM / BGM 精確 vol surface · N 參數

\[ \frac{dL_i(t)}{L_i(t)} = \mu_i^{\text{spot}}(t)\,dt + \sigma_i\,dW_i(t),\quad i=1,\ldots,N \]

\(L_i(t)\)：tenor \(i\) 的 LIBOR forward rate | \(\sigma_i\)：各 tenor 年化 vol

Spot Measure 漂移項（無套利）

\[ \mu_i^{\text{spot}} = \sum_{k=p(t)}^{i} \frac{\delta\,\sigma_k\,\sigma_i\,\rho_{ki}\,L_k}{1+\delta\,L_k} \]

\(p(t)\)：當前最短存活 tenor 索引；\(\delta\)：tenor 步長（1Y）

相關結構 + Caplet Black 公式

\[ \rho_{ij} = e^{-\lambda|i-j|},\quad\lambda\text{：衰減參數} \] \[ \text{Caplet}(K,T_i) = \delta\,P(0,T_{i+1})\bigl[L_i(0)\,N(d_1)-K\,N(d_2)\bigr] \] \[ d_{1,2} = \frac{\ln(L_i(0)/K) \pm \frac{1}{2}\sigma_i^2 T_i}{\sigma_i\sqrt{T_i}} \]

折現因子（離散複利）

\[ P(0,T_n) = \prod_{i=0}^{n-1}\frac{1}{1+\delta\,L_i(0)} \]

▶ hkrbc-esg src/lmm.py · --ir-model LMM

hkrbc-esg v2.0 支持三種 IR 模型一鍵切換： --ir-model HW1F ⭐  /  --ir-model HW2F ⭐⭐  /  --ir-model LMM ⭐⭐⭐  |  HK 建議：Par Fund TVOG → HW1F；曲線斜率建模 → HW2F；精確 vol surface → LMM
EQ Equity Return  股票總回報 校準目標：股指現貨水平 + 隱含 vol surface + 股息率曲線

① GBM / Black-Scholes 起步首選 · 1 參數

\[ dS(t) = r(t)\,S(t)\,dt + \sigma_{\!eq}\,S(t)\,dW^{\mathbb{Q}}_{eq}(t) \]

\(\sigma_{eq}\)：年化波動率（ATM 隱含 vol 校準）| Q-mode: drift = \(r(t)\)；P-mode: drift = \(r(t)+\text{ERP}\)

精確解（對數常態）

\[ S(T) = S(0)\exp\!\left[\!\int_0^T\!r(t)dt - \tfrac{\sigma^2}{2}T + \sigma\sqrt{T}\,Z\right],\quad Z\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1) \]

歐式看漲期權（Black-Scholes）

\[ C = S(0)\,N(d_1) - K\,e^{-rT}\,N(d_2) \] \[ d_{1,2} = \frac{\ln(S_0/K)+(r\pm\sigma^2/2)T}{\sigma\sqrt{T}} \]

Martingale 驗證

\[ \mathbb{E}^{\mathbb{Q}}\!\left[e^{-\int_0^T r\,ds}\cdot S(T)\right] = S(0) \;\checkmark \]

hkrbc-esg Q-mode 每次模擬後自動驗證此等式（容忍 1%）

▶ hkrbc-esg src/equity.py · Q/P 雙模式 · HSI σ≈20%

② Heston 隨機波動率 行業標準 · 5 參數

\[ dS = r\,S\,dt + \sqrt{v}\,S\,dW_1 \] \[ dv = \kappa(\theta - v)\,dt + \xi\sqrt{v}\,dW_2,\quad dW_1 dW_2=\rho\,dt \]

\(\kappa\)：回歸速度 | \(\theta\)：長期 vol² | \(\xi\)：vol-of-vol | \(v_0\)：初始 vol² | \(\rho\)：股-vol 相關（通常負）

特徵函數（半解析定價）

\[ \varphi(u,\tau) = e^{iu\ln S_0}\cdot e^{C(\tau,u)+D(\tau,u)v_0} \] \[ C = r\,iu\tau + \frac{\kappa\theta}{\xi^2}\!\left[(\kappa-\rho\xi\,iu-d)\tau - 2\ln\frac{1-g\,e^{-d\tau}}{1-g}\right] \] \[ d = \sqrt{(\rho\xi iu-\kappa)^2+\xi^2(iu+u^2)},\quad g=\frac{\kappa-\rho\xi iu-d}{\kappa-\rho\xi iu+d} \]

Feller 條件（確保 v(t)>0）

\[ 2\kappa\theta > \xi^2 \]

不滿足時 v(t) 仍為非負但可觸及 0；HSI 典型：κ=3, θ=0.04, ξ=0.5，Feller=0.24>0.25（臨界）

COS/FFT 期權定價（校準核心）

\[ C(K,T) = e^{-rT}\sum_{k=0}^{N-1}{}' \text{Re}\!\left[\varphi\!\left(\tfrac{k\pi}{b-a}\right)e^{-ika\pi/(b-a)}\right]V_k \]

\(V_k\) 為截斷 cosine 係數；校準最小化多 strike × 多 tenor 的隱含 vol 誤差

▶ Python: ql.HestonModel + ql.AnalyticHestonEngine

③ Local Vol (Dupire) 精確擬合 · 非參數

\[ dS = r\,S\,dt + \sigma_{\text{loc}}(S,t)\,S\,dW^{\mathbb{Q}}(t) \]

\(\sigma_{\text{loc}}(S,t)\) 為非參數函數，由市場 vol surface 反推；理論上無需再校準

Dupire 公式（從 C(K,T) 提取 σ_loc）

\[ \sigma_{\text{loc}}^2(K,T) = \frac{\dfrac{\partial C}{\partial T} + r\,K\,\dfrac{\partial C}{\partial K}}{\dfrac{1}{2}K^2\,\dfrac{\partial^2 C}{\partial K^2}} \]

分子 = 時間衰減 + 折現調整；分母 = Gamma（二階 strike 導數）；僅需市場 vol surface

Fokker-Planck 等價（對偶方程）

\[ \frac{\partial p}{\partial t} = \frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial^2}{\partial S^2}\!\bigl[\sigma_{\text{loc}}^2 S^2 p\bigr] - \frac{\partial}{\partial S}[r\,S\,p] \]

\(p(S,t)\) 為風險中性密度；數值求解（PDE）即得所有 strike 期權價格

局限 & SLV 改良

Forward smile 扭曲：\(\sigma_{\text{loc}}(S,t)\) 的「動態」行為與市場不符（純 LV 模型 forward vol 過平）
SLV（隨機局部波動率）：\(dS = r S dt + \sigma_{\text{loc}}(S,t)\sqrt{v}\,S\,dW_1\)，結合 Heston 優點

▶ Python: ql.LocalVolSurface；ESG 中少用純 LV，多用 SLV

Python 難度： GBM（hkrbc-esg，⭐ 開箱即用）→ Heston（QuantLib，⭐⭐ 需 vol surface）→ Local Vol（⭐⭐⭐ 需密集 vol grid）| HK 建議：Par Fund TVOG → GBM；VA/GMxB → Heston；Exotic → SLV
CS Credit Spread  信用利差 校準目標：分評級初始利差 + 歷史波動率 + Sch.6 違約率

① CIR（Cox-Ingersoll-Ross）— 主流 IG 利差模型

SDE

\[ d\,cs(t) = \kappa\bigl(\mu - cs(t)\bigr)\,dt + \sigma\sqrt{cs(t)}\,dW^{\mathbb{Q}}(t) \]

\(\kappa\)：均值回歸速度  |  \(\mu\)：長期均值  |  \(\sigma\)：波動係數  |  \(cs_0\)：初始利差

核心數學性質

① 條件分布（非中心 χ² 分布）

\[ cs(t)\,\Big|\,cs(0) \;\sim\; \frac{\sigma^2(1-e^{-\kappa t})}{4\kappa}\;\chi^2\!\left(\frac{4\kappa\mu}{\sigma^2},\;\frac{4\kappa\,cs(0)\,e^{-\kappa t}}{\sigma^2(1-e^{-\kappa t})}\right) \]

自由度 \(\nu = 4\kappa\mu/\sigma^2\)，非中心參數 \(\lambda_t = 4\kappa\,cs_0\,e^{-\kappa t}/[\sigma^2(1-e^{-\kappa t})]\)

② 均值與方差

\[ \mathbb{E}[cs(t)] = cs_0\,e^{-\kappa t} + \mu(1-e^{-\kappa t}) \] \[ \mathrm{Var}[cs(t)] = cs_0\,\frac{\sigma^2}{\kappa}\!\left(e^{-\kappa t}-e^{-2\kappa t}\right) + \frac{\mu\sigma^2}{2\kappa}(1-e^{-\kappa t})^2 \]

均值從 \(cs_0\) 指數衰減至長期均值 \(\mu\)；方差在 \(t\to\infty\) 時趨於 \(\mu\sigma^2/(2\kappa)\)

③ Feller 條件（確保嚴格正值）

\[ 2\kappa\mu > \sigma^2 \]

HKD IG 典型值：\(2\times0.15\times0.008 = 0.0024 > \sigma^2 = 0.000625\)  ✅
不滿足時 \(cs(t)\) 仍為非負但可觸及 0，hkrbc-esg 用反射邊界 \(\max(cs,0)\) 補救

④ 零息信用債券解析公式（CIR Bond Pricing）

\[ P(0,T) = A(T)\,\exp\!\bigl(-B(T)\cdot cs_0\bigr) \]

\[ B(T) = \frac{2(e^{\gamma T}-1)}{(\gamma+\kappa)(e^{\gamma T}-1)+2\gamma}, \quad \gamma = \sqrt{\kappa^2+2\sigma^2} \] \[ A(T) = \left[\frac{2\gamma\,e^{(\kappa+\gamma)T/2}}{(\gamma+\kappa)(e^{\gamma T}-1)+2\gamma}\right]^{\!\tfrac{2\kappa\mu}{\sigma^2}} \]

用途：直接計算信用利差曲線對應的折現因子；校準時最小化 \(\sum_i[P_{\text{model}}(T_i)-P_{\text{market}}(T_i)]^2\)

⑤ 離散時間 MLE 校準（歷史利差序列）

設 \(\Delta t = 1/12\)（月），觀測序列 \(\{cs_0,cs_1,\ldots,cs_n\}\)，條件密度為非中心 \(\chi^2\)，對數似然： \[ \ell(\kappa,\mu,\sigma) = \sum_{i=1}^{n}\ln f_{\chi^2}\!\left(\frac{4\kappa\,cs_i}{\sigma^2(1-e^{-\kappa\Delta t})}\bigg|\,\nu,\,\lambda_i\right) - n\ln\frac{\sigma^2(1-e^{-\kappa\Delta t})}{4\kappa} \]

Python：scipy.optimize.minimize 最大化 \(\ell\)；初始值可用 OLS（\(\Delta cs_i = \alpha + \beta\,cs_{i-1} + \varepsilon_i\)，其中 \(\kappa\approx-\beta/\Delta t\)，\(\mu\approx\alpha/(\kappa\Delta t)\)）

▶ hkrbc-esg v2.0 已實現 src/cir.py（Euler-Maruyama + Feller 校驗 + 反射邊界）

② Jump-Diffusion（CIR + Jumps）— 捕捉信用事件

SDE（CIR 基礎 + Poisson 跳躍）

\[ d\,cs(t) = \kappa(\mu-cs)\,dt + \sigma\sqrt{cs}\,dW(t) + J\,dN_t(\lambda) \]

\(N_t(\lambda)\)：強度為 \(\lambda\) 的 Poisson 計數過程  |  \(J\)：跳躍幅度隨機變量

① 跳躍幅度分布（Merton vs Kou）

Merton（對稱跳躍）

\[ J \sim \mathcal{N}(\mu_J, \sigma_J^2) \]

跳幅服從正態分布
可正可負（利差升降均有）

Kou（不對稱雙指數）

\[ f_J(x) = p\eta_1 e^{-\eta_1 x}\mathbf{1}_{x\geq0} + (1-p)\eta_2 e^{\eta_2 x}\mathbf{1}_{x<0} \]

\(p\)：上跳概率；\(\eta_1,\eta_2\)：各向衰減率
更好捕捉利差不對稱跳升

② 均值與方差（含跳躍項）

\[ \mathbb{E}[cs(t)] = \mathbb{E}_{\text{CIR}}[cs(t)] + \lambda\,\mathbb{E}[J]\cdot\frac{1-e^{-\kappa t}}{\kappa} \] \[ \mathrm{Var}[cs(t)] = \mathrm{Var}_{\text{CIR}} + \lambda\,\mathbb{E}[J^2]\cdot\frac{1-e^{-2\kappa t}}{2\kappa} \]

跳躍使長期方差增加 \(\lambda\mathbb{E}[J^2]/(2\kappa)\)；若 \(\mathbb{E}[J]>0\)（上跳為主），長期均值也上移

③ CDS 公平利差（簡化，違約強度 = cs(t)）

\[ \text{CDS spread} = \frac{(1-R)\int_0^T e^{-rt}\,\mathbb{E}\!\left[cs(t)\,e^{-\int_0^t cs(s)ds}\right]dt}{\int_0^T e^{-rt}\,\mathbb{E}\!\left[e^{-\int_0^t cs(s)ds}\right]dt} \]

\(R\)：回收率（Recovery Rate，通常 40%）；分子為期望違約損失，分母為保費腿 DV01
CIR 下分母有解析解（CIR Bond formula）；含跳躍時需 MC 估值

④ 跳躍參數校準（HK 市場挑戰）

跳躍強度 \(\lambda\) 可由歷史利差序列的「異常大幅變動」頻率估計： \[ \hat{\lambda} = \frac{\#\{|\Delta cs_i| > c\cdot\hat{\sigma}_{\text{CIR}}\}}{n\cdot\Delta t}, \quad c\approx 3\text{–}4 \] 跳幅均值 \(\hat{\mu}_J\) 和 \(\hat{\sigma}_J\) 由篩選出的異常樣本估計。
HK 挑戰：IG 債券市場深度有限，異常事件少，\(\lambda\) 估計誤差大，通常假借 US/EUR IG 歷史參數 

# CIR + Poisson 跳躍離散化（Euler-Maruyama）
cs_cur = max(cs[i], 0)
# CIR 擴散項
cs_next = cs_cur + kappa*(mu - cs_cur)*dt + sigma*sqrt(cs_cur*dt)*Z[i]
# Poisson 跳躍（每步期望 λ·Δt 次跳躍）
n_jumps = np.random.poisson(lam * dt)
if n_jumps > 0:
    # Kou 雙指數跳幅（p=上跳概率）
    J_sizes = np.where(np.random.uniform(size=n_jumps) < p,
                       np.random.exponential(1/eta1, n_jumps),
                      -np.random.exponential(1/eta2, n_jumps))
    cs_next += J_sizes.sum()
cs[i+1] = max(cs_next, 0)   # 反射邊界

▶ Python：基於 hkrbc-esg src/cir.py 加入跳躍層即可；跳躍參數可在 esg_config.yaml 擴展配置

模型選擇建議

HKRBC TVOG / IG 信用組合 → CIR
HY 債券 / CDS 定價 → CIR + Jumps
歷史數據充分時用 MLE；否則用 OLS + Feller 校驗

HKD IG 典型參數

\(\kappa = 0.15\)，\(\mu = 0.008\)（80bps），\(\sigma = 0.025\)
\(cs_0 = 0.0085\)（85bps，2024 水平）
Feller：\(2\kappa\mu = 0.0024 > \sigma^2 = 0.000625\) ✅
穩態方差：\(\mu\sigma^2/(2\kappa) \approx 5.6 \times 10^{-5}\)（7.5bps 標準差）

與 HKRBC 的關聯

信用利差路徑影響 MA 組合的 earned rate：
\(\text{earned rate}(t) \approx r_f(t) + cs(t) - \text{EL}(t)\)
EL（Expected Loss）= 違約強度 × (1 − R)
高利差情景 → 資產端收益高 → AS 增長更快 → GSV 觸發概率降低

FX FX Rate  外匯匯率 校準目標：FX forward curve + ATM FX option vol

① Garman-Kohlhagen — Q-mode 標配

SDE（風險中性測度）

\[ dS_{fx}(t) = \bigl(r_d(t)-r_f(t)\bigr)S_{fx}(t)\,dt + \sigma_{fx}\,S_{fx}(t)\,dW^{\mathbb{Q}}_{fx}(t) \]

\(r_d\)：本幣無風險利率 | \(r_f\)：外幣無風險利率 | \(\sigma_{fx}\)：FX 波動率

精確解（對數常態）

\[ S_{fx}(T) = S_{fx}(0)\exp\!\left[\int_0^T(r_d-r_f)\,dt - \tfrac{\sigma_{fx}^2}{2}T + \sigma_{fx}\sqrt{T}\,Z\right] \]

無拋補利率平價（UIP）—— 遠期匯率

\[ F(0,T) = S_{fx}(0)\cdot e^{(r_d - r_f)T} \]

Martingale（本幣折現）：\(\mathbb{E}^{\mathbb{Q}}\!\left[e^{-\int_0^T r_d\,ds}S_{fx}(T)\right]=S_{fx}(0)\,e^{-\int_0^T r_f\,ds}\) ✓

Garman-Kohlhagen FX 歐式看漲期權

\[ C_{fx} = S_{fx}(0)\,e^{-r_f T}N(d_1) - K\,e^{-r_d T}N(d_2) \] \[ d_{1,2} = \frac{\ln(S_0/K)+(r_d-r_f\pm\sigma_{fx}^2/2)T}{\sigma_{fx}\sqrt{T}} \]

與 BS 公式結構相同，\(r_f\) 替代連續股息率

HKD/USD 聯繫匯率制度（特殊情形）

HKMA 將 HKD/USD 維持在 7.75–7.85 區間；等效 \(\sigma_{HKD/USD}\approx 0.1\%\)（極小）
ESG 中通常設 \(S_{HKD/USD}(t)\equiv 7.80\) 或建模為帶反射邊界的 GBM： \[ dS = \sigma_{fx}\,S\,dW,\quad S(t)\in[7.75,\,7.85] \] HKD/RMB、HKD/EUR 則用標準 GK 模型，\(\sigma\approx 3\%\text{–}5\%\)

▶ hkrbc-esg v2.0 src/fx.py（利率平價 drift，HKD/USD σ=0.1%）

② FX Heston / Stochastic Vol — 精確 vol smile

SDE（FX + 隨機 vol）

\[ dS_{fx} = (r_d-r_f)S_{fx}\,dt + \sqrt{v}\,S_{fx}\,dW_1 \] \[ dv = \kappa_v(\theta_v-v)\,dt + \xi_v\sqrt{v}\,dW_2,\quad dW_1 dW_2=\rho_v\,dt \]

結構與股票 Heston 完全對應，但 drift 改為 \(r_d-r_f\)

Brigo-Mercurio 三因子模型（IR + FX 聯合）

完整 MA 組合需要 IR（HW1F/HW2F）+ FX（GK）聯合模型： \[ dS_{fx} = (r_d(t)-r_f(t))S_{fx}\,dt + \sigma_{fx}\,S_{fx}\,dW_{fx} \] 相關矩陣 \(\rho_{r_d,fx}\)、\(\rho_{r_f,fx}\) 由歷史利率-匯率聯合時序估計

Triangular Arbitrage 約束（多幣種）

\[ S_{A/C}(t) = S_{A/B}(t)\cdot S_{B/C}(t) \]

多幣種 ESG 中 FX 路徑須滿足三角套利無套利條件；通過共享 Cholesky 因子實現

校準 — ATM + RR + BF（vol smile）

FX vol 通常以 Delta（25D/10D）報價：
ATM（平值）+ RR（Risk Reversal，偏斜）+ BF（Butterfly，峰度）
Vanna-Volga 方法可從三個 vol 報價重建完整 smile；HK 市場 FX option 流動性集中在 USD/RMB

HK 典型 FX 參數

HKD/USD：\(\sigma\approx 0.1\%\)（聯繫匯率，幾乎固定）
HKD/RMB：\(\sigma\approx 3\%\text{–}4\%\)（輕度管控，歷史年化）
HKD/EUR：\(\sigma\approx 7\%\text{–}9\%\)（浮動，歷史年化）

▶ 進階：ql.Heston + ql.GarmanKohlagenProcess；多幣種共用 Cholesky

HK 建議：HKD/USD 聯繫匯率可簡化為常數 7.80；HKD/RMB 用 GK（σ≈3%）；多幣種 MA 組合需聯合 IR+FX Cholesky
PR Property Return  房地產回報 校準目標：REIT 歷史回報 / Centaline Property Index 時序

① Equity-Proxy 代理模型 — 主流 HK 實現

對數回報代理公式

\[ r_{prop}(t) = \beta\cdot r_{eq}(t-\ell) + \varepsilon(t),\quad \varepsilon\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma_{idio}^2\Delta t) \]

\(\beta\)：beta 係數（通常 0.4–0.7）| \(\ell\)：時間滯後步數（lag 1–2 年）| \(\sigma_{idio}\)：特殊波動率

房地產指數路徑（指數積累）

\[ \text{Prop}(t+\Delta t) = \text{Prop}(t)\cdot\exp\!\bigl[\beta\cdot\ln\tfrac{S_{eq}(t-\ell)}{S_{eq}(t-\ell-\Delta t)} + \varepsilon(t)\bigr] \]

OLS 校準（歷史時序）

\[ \hat{\beta} = \frac{\text{Cov}(r_{prop,t},\,r_{eq,t-\ell})}{\text{Var}(r_{eq,t-\ell})},\quad \hat{\sigma}_{idio}^2 = \frac{1}{n-2}\sum_{i}(r_{prop,i}-\hat\beta\,r_{eq,i-\ell})^2 \]

最優 lag \(\ell^*\) 由最大化 \(R^2\) 確定；HK REIT 歷史數據期限有限，通常補充 HK CPI 修正後 Price Index

殘差方差分解（解釋力）

\[ R^2 = \frac{\beta^2\,\mathrm{Var}(r_{eq})}{\beta^2\,\mathrm{Var}(r_{eq})+\sigma_{idio}^2\Delta t} \]

HK 典型：β=0.6，lag=4步（季），σ_idio=8%/yr，R²≈55%

與 HKRBC 的關聯

MA 組合中 Investment Property 的公允值影響 AS 計算：
\(\text{AS}(t) \propto \text{Prop}(t)\cdot w_{prop}\) ——房地產暴跌情景下 TVOG 顯著上升
壓力測試：β 增至 0.9（危機期間 beta 收斂）+ 去除 lag（即時傳導）

▶ hkrbc-esg v2.0 src/property_return.py（β=0.6，lag=4，σ_idio=8%）

② Regime-Switching 狀態切換模型 — 房地產週期

條件 GBM（狀態依賴參數）

\[ r_{prop}(t) = \mu_{s_t}\Delta t + \sigma_{s_t}\sqrt{\Delta t}\,Z(t) \] \[ s_t\in\{{\uparrow}\text{上行},\;\text{平}\text{穩},\;{\downarrow}\text{下行}\} \]

狀態由一階馬爾可夫鏈驅動；各狀態均值 \(\mu_s\) 和波動率 \(\sigma_s\) 獨立估計

馬爾可夫轉移矩陣（HK 房地產歷史）

\[ P = \begin{pmatrix} p_{\uparrow\uparrow} & p_{\uparrow 0} & p_{\uparrow\downarrow} \\ p_{0\uparrow} & p_{00} & p_{0\downarrow} \\ p_{\downarrow\uparrow} & p_{\downarrow 0} & p_{\downarrow\downarrow} \end{pmatrix} \approx \begin{pmatrix} 0.85 & 0.12 & 0.03 \\ 0.10 & 0.82 & 0.08 \\ 0.04 & 0.16 & 0.80 \end{pmatrix} \]

轉移概率由 Centaline Property Index 月度數據（1993–2024）MLE 估計

穩態分布（各狀態佔比）

\[ \pi = \pi P \;\Rightarrow\; \pi_{\uparrow}\approx 45\%,\;\pi_{0}\approx 38\%,\;\pi_{\downarrow}\approx 17\% \]

HK 房地產歷史上行期比例較高，但下行期衝擊更劇烈（SARS/2008/2019）

各狀態典型參數（HK Property）

上行：\(\mu_{\uparrow}=+12\%/\text{yr}\)，\(\sigma_{\uparrow}=10\%\)
平穩：\(\mu_{0}=+2\%/\text{yr}\)，\(\sigma_{0}=6\%\)
下行：\(\mu_{\downarrow}=-15\%/\text{yr}\)，\(\sigma_{\downarrow}=18\%\)（尖峰厚尾）

Hamilton EM 算法（MLE 校準）

\[ \hat{P},\{\hat\mu_s,\hat\sigma_s\} = \arg\max_{\theta}\sum_t\ln p(r_{prop,t}\mid s_t;\theta) \]

Python：hmmlearn.GaussianHMM（3 states，full covariance）

▶ Python: hmmlearn.GaussianHMM；hkrbc-esg v2.0 實現代理模型（Proxy to Equity）

HK 建議：首選 Equity-Proxy（數據可得性好）；TVOG 壓測用 Regime-Switching（捕捉週期峰谷）；兩種方法可並行驗證
INF Inflation  通脹 / 費用指數 校準目標：HK CPI 歷史序列 + 通脹互換（若可得）

① OU 通脹過程 — 主流簡化（hkrbc-esg 實現）

SDE（即時通脹率）

\[ d\pi(t) = \kappa_\pi\bigl(\mu_\pi - \pi(t)\bigr)\,dt + \sigma_\pi\,dW_\pi(t) \]

\(\kappa_\pi\)：回歸速度 | \(\mu_\pi\)：長期均值（HK 目標 2%–3%）| \(\sigma_\pi\)：波動率

精確解（高斯過程）

\[ \pi(t) = \mu_\pi + (\pi_0-\mu_\pi)e^{-\kappa_\pi t} + \sigma_\pi\!\int_0^t e^{-\kappa_\pi(t-s)}dW_\pi(s) \]

條件分布（正態）

\[ \pi(t)\,\big|\,\pi(0) \;\sim\; \mathcal{N}\!\left(\mu_\pi+(π_0-\mu_\pi)e^{-\kappa_\pi t},\;\frac{\sigma_\pi^2}{2\kappa_\pi}(1-e^{-2\kappa_\pi t})\right) \]

穩態（\(t\to\infty\)）：\(\pi_\infty\sim\mathcal{N}(\mu_\pi,\,\sigma_\pi^2/(2\kappa_\pi))\)；HK 典型穩態標準差 ≈ 2.4%

CPI 指數路徑（積分累積）

\[ \text{CPI}(T) = \text{CPI}(0)\cdot\exp\!\left(\int_0^T\pi(t)\,dt\right) \] \[ \mathbb{E}\!\left[\int_0^T\pi(t)dt\right] = \mu_\pi T + (\pi_0-\mu_\pi)\frac{1-e^{-\kappa_\pi T}}{\kappa_\pi} \]

積分為高斯，故 CPI(T) 為對數常態分布

CPI(T) 的方差（積分 OU 方差）

\[ \mathrm{Var}\!\left[\int_0^T\!\pi\,dt\right] = \frac{\sigma_\pi^2}{\kappa_\pi^2}\!\left[T-\frac{2(1-e^{-\kappa_\pi T})}{\kappa_\pi}+\frac{1-e^{-2\kappa_\pi T}}{2\kappa_\pi}\right] \]

MLE 校準（HK CPI 月度序列）

\[ \hat{\kappa}_\pi \approx -\frac{\ln\hat\rho_1}{\Delta t},\quad \hat\mu_\pi = \bar\pi,\quad \hat\sigma_\pi^2 = \frac{2\hat\kappa_\pi\cdot\hat s^2}{1-e^{-2\hat\kappa_\pi\Delta t}} \]

\(\hat\rho_1\)：序列一階自相關 | \(\hat s^2\)：殘差樣本方差 | HK CPI 數據：Census & Statistics Dept（月度）

▶ hkrbc-esg v2.0 src/inflation.py（π₀=2%，μ=3%，κ=0.20，σ=1.5%）

② Jarrow-Yildirim 雙因子 — 通脹衍生品定價

三因子架構（名義 + 實際 + CPI）

\[ dr_n = (\theta_n(t)-a_n r_n)\,dt+\sigma_n\,dW_n \quad\text{（名義利率，HW1F）} \] \[ dr_r = (\theta_r(t)-a_r r_r - \rho_{rI}\sigma_r\sigma_I)\,dt+\sigma_r\,dW_r \quad\text{（實際利率，含 Ito 修正）} \] \[ \frac{dI(t)}{I(t)} = (r_n-r_r)\,dt + \sigma_I\,dW_I \quad\text{（CPI 指數）} \]

\(I(t)\)：CPI 指數 | Fisher 方程：實際利率 \(r_r = r_n - \pi\)（近似）

通脹掛鈎零息債券（ZCIIS）定價

\[ V(0,T) = P_n(0,T)\cdot\mathbb{E}^{\mathbb{Q}_n}\!\left[\frac{I(T)}{I(0)}\right] = \frac{P_r(0,T)}{P_n(0,T)}\cdot P_n(0,T) = P_r(0,T) \]

\(P_r(0,T)\)：實際利率下的零息債券價格；通脹互換可直接由實際利率曲線定價

通脹期限結構（Break-even Inflation）

\[ \text{BEI}(0,T) = \frac{P_n(0,T)}{P_r(0,T)} - 1 \approx \int_0^T\mathbb{E}[\pi(t)]\,\frac{dt}{T} \]

由名義利率曲線（Sch.4）與實際利率曲線（ILB/IRS）共同確定

HK 市場限制 & 替代方案

HK 通脹掛鈎債券市場極薄（主要為 iBond，零售產品），實際利率曲線難以精確校準
替代方案：
① 借用英國 RPI 或美國 TIPS 曲線形狀，平移至 HK CPI 均值
② OU 通脹模型直接以 CPI 歷史月度數據校準（hkrbc-esg 實現方式）
③ 將通脹設為利率的線性函數（最簡化）：\(\pi(t) = a + b\cdot r_n(t) + \varepsilon\)

通脹與 HKRBC 費用假設（Cap. 41R Rule 5）

HKRBC 規定費用假設需含通脹調整：
\[\text{Expense}(t) = \text{Expense}(0)\cdot\frac{\text{CPI}(t)}{\text{CPI}(0)}\] 情景下通脹衝擊（如 CPI 突升至 8%）直接增加 BEL 中的費用負債，提高 TVOG 中的路徑費用

▶ Python: JY 模型需 QuantLib 或自行實現；hkrbc-esg 用 OU 簡化版

HK 建議：HKRBC TVOG 費用通脹 → OU 模型（hkrbc-esg）；通脹掛鈎產品 → Jarrow-Yildirim（需借 GBP/USD ILB 曲線校準）

Calibration Methodology 校準方法

The ESG must be simultaneously market-consistent and arbitrage-free. Calibration is typically a 4-step process:

  1. Yield Curve Bootstrapping: Build the RFR curve from swap quotes / government bonds (Sch. 4 Table 1 prescribed curves for HKD, USD, RMB, etc.). Apply Smith-Wilson or Nelson-Siegel-Svensson interpolation beyond Last Liquid Point.
  2. Cross-Asset Correlation: Estimate covariance matrix from historical data; apply Cholesky decomposition L LT = Σ. Use correlated standard normals Z* = L · Z to drive correlated Brownian motions.
  3. Parameter Calibration: Minimise sum of squared errors between model-implied prices and market prices: $$\min_{\theta} \sum_k \left( P_k^{model}(\theta) - P_k^{market} \right)^2$$ Typically calibrated separately per asset class, then validated for cross-asset consistency.
  4. Scenario Generation: Monte Carlo simulation, typically 5,000-10,000 scenarios × 100 years × monthly time steps. Use antithetic variates and low-discrepancy sequences (Sobol) for variance reduction.

Validation Framework 驗證框架

The IA requires ESG validation along five dimensions IA Circular 12 Jun 2024, App. 1:

TestWhat it checksAcceptance Criterion
Martingale Test Discounted asset prices should equal initial value in expectation: E[PT/BT] = P0 Within 0.5% of initial price; t-stat < 2
PV Test Average present value of zero-coupon bond = market price Within 1 bp at each maturity
Market Price Test Re-price calibration instruments (e.g. swaptions): model vs market Within 5% of market price
Moment Test Empirical mean / variance / skew of generated scenarios match model parameters Within 2 standard errors
Distribution Test Quantile-quantile plot for log-returns; tail behaviour reasonable No systematic deviation in tails (1%/99%)

Industry Toolkit 行業常用工具

  • Commercial ESG:
    • Moody's B&H Economic Scenario Generator (most common in Asia/HK)
    • Conning GEMS
    • WTW STAR ESG
    • Milliman MG-ALFA ESG module
  • Open Source / Self-Built: Python (QuantLib-Python) + custom calibration scripts; only feasible for small/medium insurers
  • Typical Cost: Commercial license HKD 500K-2M/year; self-build saves cost but requires PhD-level quant team

Why ESG Matters for HKRBC

  • TVOG (Rule 19): Mandatory for contracts with contractual options or financial guarantees (e.g. minimum crediting rate, surrender guarantees). TVOG = stochastic CE − deterministic CE. See §9.6 for full calculation.
  • Par fund dividend modelling: Crediting rate is path-dependent on investment return; needs stochastic scenarios.
  • Universal Life: Account value crediting linked to dynamic investment return.
  • Not used for PCR: HKRBC uses deterministic stresses for PCR (unlike Solvency II internal models). ESG is only for valuation of options & guarantees within the EBS.

Risk-Neutral vs Real-World — 兩套情景有何不同？

Risk-Neutral 風險中性Real-World 真實世界
核心問題 「這個期權/保證今天的公平價值是多少？」 「未來10年內，公司的資產負債缺口有多大？」
Drift（漂移率） 所有資產的預期回報率 = 無風險利率（risk-free rate）。
股票、債券漲跌已被"風險溢價"移除。		 資產的預期回報率 = 無風險利率 + 風險溢價（equity risk premium ≈ 3–5%）。
反映投資者真實預期。
校準目標 市場可觀察價格（swaption vol surface、equity implied vol）。
Martingale test：\(E^Q[B(T)^{-1} \cdot S(T)] = S(0)\)		 歷史時間序列（過去20–30年利率、股票回報）＋宏觀經濟假設。
直覺理解 在風險中性世界，所有投資者都是風險中立的，因此股票和現金的預期回報相同（= 無風險利率）。這是一個「定價用的虛擬世界」，並非現實。 在真實世界，投資者厭惡風險，股票必須提供高於無風險利率的超額回報，才能吸引人持有。這才是「現實世界」。
HKRBC 用途 TVOG（Rule 19）、EBS 負債估值
→ 市場一致性估值，無套利		 ALM 壓力測試、ORSA、SAA 優化
→ 評估長期持續性和資本充足率
典型參數差異（以利率為例） 初始 yield curve = IA Sch. 4 無風險曲線；mean reversion → risk-free forward curve 初始 yield curve = 相同；但長期 mean reversion level 可能更高（加入 term premium）

一句話總結： Risk-neutral 世界裡，每條路徑的加權平均（用 discount factor 貼現）等於今天的市場價格——這是 TVOG 計算的基礎。 Real-world 世界裡，路徑的平均是公司的真實預期——這是 ALM / 壓力測試的基礎。 兩者用同一個 ESG 引擎，但漂移率（drift）不同。

實操建模：具體哪些假設不同，如何設置？

從數學上看，兩個測度之間的橋樑是 市場風險價格（Market Price of Risk，λ）。 根據 Girsanov 定理，真實世界布朗運動與風險中性布朗運動的關係為： \[ dW^P = dW^Q + \lambda \, dt \] 即將 drift 加上 λ·σ，便從 Q-measure 切換到 P-measure。實操中不需要顯式算 λ，而是直接在 ESG 參數表裡替換下面這些假設：

風險因子Risk-Neutral（Q-measure）假設Real-World（P-measure）假設實操調整方式
利率
（Hull-White）		 長期均值 θ(t) = 當前 forward curve（IA Sch. 4）
確保模型精確 fit 初始 yield curve
κ, σ 由 swaption vol 校準		 長期均值 θ(t) = forward curve + term premium（約 0.5–1.5%）
反映投資者對長期持有利率風險的補償

⚠️ higher ≠ free lunch：term premium 是對利率上行風險的補償——利率上行路徑中，現有長債 MtM 損失 ≈ duration × Δr（例如 7yr × 0.5% = −3.5%），部分抵消再投資收益。P-measure 預測更樂觀，但方差也更大。		 在 ESG 參數文件中調高 long-run mean；κ 和 σ 通常保持不變（波動結構相同）

⚠️ term premium 估計高度不確定（歷史範圍 −0.5% 至 +2%），是 P-measure 參數的主要不確定來源
股票
（GBM）		 Drift μ = r（無風險利率）
σ 由 ATM implied vol 校準
martingale test：\(E^Q[e^{-rT} S_T] = S_0\)		 Drift μ = r + ERP（股票風險溢價，約 3–5%）
σ 用歷史實現波動率（可與 implied vol 不同）		 在 ESG 配置中把 equity drift 從 r 改為 r + ERP；σ 可用歷史 vol 替換 implied vol
信用利差
（Credit Spread）		 初始 spread = 市場觀察值（含風險溢價）
均值回歸至市場 long-run spread		 預期 spread = 預期信用損失（EL = PD × LGD）
去除風險溢價部分，均值回歸至歷史平均		 調低 long-run spread 均值（剔除 credit risk premium）；或用歷史回歸估計 P-measure 參數
匯率
（FX）		 Drift = 利率平價（interest rate parity）
即 μ_FX = r_domestic − r_foreign		 Drift = 購買力平價 / 宏觀匯率預測
（可能偏離利率平價）		 替換 drift 假設；σ_FX 通常相同

校準流程對比：

步驟Risk-Neutral 校準Real-World 校準
數據來源市場報價：swaption vol surface、equity implied vol、CDS spread歷史時間序列：20–30年利率、股票指數月度回報、信用損失率
校準目標Martingale test 通過：\(E^Q[e^{-\int r\,dt} S_T] = S_0\)最小化歷史路徑模擬誤差（MLE / Kalman Filter）
關鍵輸出κ（mean reversion speed）、σ（vol）、θ(t)（drift term）μ_equity（含 ERP）、長期利率 target、歷史 vol
主要工具Optimizer（最小化 swaption 價格誤差）OLS / VAR / Kalman Filter（歷史回歸）
常見軟件ESG 廠商（Barrie & Hibbert、Moody's AA）內建優化器R / Python statsmodels；或廠商 P-measure module

在 Prophet / ESG 軟件的實際操作： 通常 ESG 引擎有兩套參數配置文件（parameter set）： 一套標記為 Q_params.xml（risk-neutral，用於 TVOG run）； 另一套標記為 P_params.xml（real-world，用於 ALM / ORSA run）。 切換時只需在 Prophet 的 ESG 呼叫處更換參數文件，模型結構（HW2F、GBM 等）完全相同。

ℹ️ 為什麼 TVOG 必須用 Q-measure？ FTAP 無套利定理、Q-measure martingale 條件、Rule 19 監管要求 → 詳見 §10.1 TVOG Calculation Deep-Dive。

§8.A ESG From-Scratch Methodology — Practitioner Step-by-Step Guide 從零實作方法論

本節為 實作層面的完整方法論，基於開源項目 hkrbc-esg， 逐步解析如何構建 ESG。 同一套代碼框架，通過切換一個參數（mode: Q / mode: P），支持兩種完全不同的精算用途：

§8.A-Q  |  HKRBC TVOG 模式（Q-measure）

用途：HKRBC Cap. 41R Rule 19 TVOG、IFRS 17 MRB
本質：風險中性定價，情景是「市場一致」的，不是真實概率
核心條件：通過 martingale test（債券和股票）
利率漂移：drift = θ(t)，精確拟合 IA Sch.4 yield curve，term_premium=0
股票漂移：drift = r(t)（無風險利率），equity_risk_premium=0
情景數：≥1,000（Rule 19 要求） 

mode: Q
hull_white_1f:
  term_premium_Q: 0.000  # 精確拟合市場
equity:
  equity_risk_premium_Q: 0.000  # 無超額回報
simulation:
  n_scenarios: 1000

▶ 运行：python examples/run_esg.py --ir-model HW1F（自動 martingale test）

▶ 輸出：esg_scenarios.csv（10列：rfr_short / disc_factor / eq_total_return / credit_spread / fx_rate / prop_return / cpi_index / inflation_rate）

§8.A-P  |  ALM / ORSA 模式（P-measure）

用途：ALM 壓力測試、SAA 策略分析、ORSA 前瞻性資本評估
本質：真實世界概率，情景包含市場風險溢價
核心條件：情景分布應與歷史數據吻合（不需要 martingale test）
利率漂移：drift = θ(t) + a×λ，長端均值 = forward rate + term_premium（~80bps）
股票漂移：drift = r(t) + ERP，長期預期回報 ≈ 3.5% + 5.5% = 9%
情景數：100–500 條已足夠（無最低法規要求） 

mode: P
hull_white_1f:
  term_premium_P: 0.008  # 80bps 期限溢價
equity:
  equity_risk_premium_P: 0.055  # 5.5% ERP
simulation:
  n_scenarios: 200

▶ 运行：python examples/run_esg.py --mode P --scenarios 200（自動跳過 martingale test）

▶ 同樣輸出全部 10 列因子（IR/EQ/CS/FX/PR/INF），P-measure 下股票 30Y 均值 ≈ 5.8×（vs Q-mode ≈ 2.9×）

§8.A 代碼模塊對應（兩種模式共用框架）
步驟 概念原理 代碼文件 Q-mode（TVOG） P-mode（ALM/ORSA）
§8.A.0 模式選擇 確定用途：TVOG 定價 or 真實世界預測 esg_config.yaml mode: Q mode: P

§8.A.2 利率參數 a, σ 由 swaption vol 校準（Q）或歷史數據估算（P） esg_config.yaml a=0.05, σ=0.01（市場校準） a=0.05, σ=0.01（通常相同）
§8.A.3 利率漂移 α(t) 决定長端均值收斂點 src/hw1f.py term_premium_Q=0
長端 → f(0,t)		 term_premium_P=0.008
長端 → f(0,t)+80bps
§8.A.4 股票漂移 GBM drift 決定股票長期預期回報 src/equity.py equity_risk_premium_Q=0
E[回報] ≈ r(t)≈3.5%		 equity_risk_premium_P=0.055
E[回報] ≈ r(t)+5.5%≈9%

§8.A.4e 通脹 OU 均值回歸，CPI 指數路徑 src/inflation.py π(0)=2%，μ=3%，κ=0.20，σ=1.5%
§8.A.5 情景生成 Monte Carlo + 對偶變量 + Cholesky 相關性 examples/run_esg.py
src/cholesky_corr.py		 ≥1,000 條 100–500 條
§8.A.6 驗證 Martingale Test（Q 必須通過；P 跳過） src/martingale_test.py ✅ 自動執行，
不通過則需重新校準		 ⏭ 自動跳過，
改用歷史分布比對
§8.A.7 輸出 CSV + Prophet .ESC 格式 src/scenario_output.py output/esg_scenarios.csv / .ESC（相同格式）

完整代碼：github.com/woming98/hkrbc-esg  |  只需修改 config/esg_config.yaml 頂部的 mode 字段，其餘代碼不動。

以下各子節逐步說明每個決策點的原理。§8.A-Q 完整 TVOG 操作案例已移至 §10.2，本節專注 ESG 構建方法論。§8.A-P 說明 ALM/ORSA 的 P-measure 差異。

§8.A-Q — HKRBC TVOG 模式（Q-measure）
完整操作案例（GSV Put 4.32、Prophet BEL、martingale test）已移至 §10.2 Par Fund TVOG Deep-Dive。 本節僅描述 Q-mode 的核心設定，詳細步驟見 §10。

§8.A-P   ALM / ORSA 模式（P-measure / 真實世界）

適用於：資產負債管理（ALM）壓力測試、戰略資產配置（SAA）、ORSA 前瞻性資本評估、董事會風險報告。
監管立場：HKRBC 規定 TVOG 必須用 Q-measure；ALM/ORSA 在內部使用，監管允許用 P-measure。

Q vs P：根本差異在於「漂移項（drift）」

比較維度Q-measure（TVOG）P-measure（ALM/ORSA）
利率長期均值 收斂至 IA Sch.4 遠期利率 f(0,t)
確保折現因子為鞅		 收斂至 f(0,t) + term_premium（如 +80bps）
反映投資者要求持有長債的額外補償
股票期望年化回報 ≈ r(t) ≈ 3%–4%（無超額回報）
Q-measure 下股票預期回報 = 無風險利率		 ≈ r(t) + ERP ≈ 9%（加股票風險溢價）
反映歷史長期股票超額回報 5%–7%
Martingale Test ✅ 必須通過（HKRBC Rule 19） ⏭ 跳過（P-measure 下期望值 ≠ 市場價格，正常）
情景驗證方式 數學驗證：E^Q[disc × Bond] = 1 統計驗證：情景均值/波動率/分位數與歷史數據吻合
典型應用 Par Fund TVOG、GMxB MRB、IFRS 17 CSM 釋放 利率敏感性分析、股債比優化、5年資本展望、壓力測試

P-mode 配置方法（只需修改 3 個參數）

# config/esg_config.yaml — 切換至 P-mode（ALM/ORSA）

mode: P   # ← 改成 P，即啟動真實世界模式

simulation:
  n_scenarios: 200   # ALM 通常 100–500 條即可，無最低法規要求
  projection_years: 30

hull_white_1f:
  a: 0.05
  sigma: 0.010
  term_premium_P: 0.008   # ← 80bps 期限溢價（典型 HKD 估算值）
                           #   來源：HKMA 研究 / ACM 期限溢價模型
                           #   代表投資者持有 10Y vs 1Y 債券要求的額外補償

equity:
  sigma_eq: 0.20
  equity_risk_premium_P: 0.055   # ← 5.5% ERP（股票風險溢價）
                                  #   來源：Damodaran 全球 ERP 估算 / 歷史 HSI 超額回報
                                  #   代表投資股票比持有現金額外獲得的年化回報

P-mode 執行輸出（與 Q-mode 對比）

$ python examples/run_esg.py
====================================================================
  HKRBC ESG Generator v1.1
  Q-mode: HKRBC TVOG / IFRS 17 MRB（風險中性）
  P-mode: ALM / SAA / ORSA（真實世界）
====================================================================
[模式] P-measure（真實世界）→ ALM / SAA / ORSA

[HW1F] a=0.05, σ=0.01, term_premium=0.0080
       正在生成 200 條利率路徑...
       完成。r(0)=0.0326，長端均值比 Q-mode 高 0.8%
       ← 即長期利率中樞比 Q-mode 高 80bps，反映期限溢價

[Equity] σ_eq=20%, ERP=5.5% | 正在生成股票路徑...
         完成。期望年化回報 ≈ r(0) + ERP = 8.76%
         ← Q-mode 的期望回報只有 3.26%，兩者相差 5.5% ERP

[Martingale Test] P-mode：跳過（Real-World 情景的期望值 ≠ 市場價格，這是正常的）
  P-mode 驗證方式：比較情景分布與歷史數據（均值、波動率、分位數）是否吻合。

✅ ESG 生成完成。請檢查 output/ 目錄下的情景文件。

P-mode 典型 ALM 應用：利率敏感性分析（Duration Gap）

用 P-mode 的 200 條情景，計算 Par Fund 在不同利率路徑下的 NAV（淨資產值）分布：

  1. 每條情景提供一條 r(t) 路徑 → 按市值計算債券資產組合現值（Assets BV）
  2. 同一條情景提供 AS 路徑 → 計算 BEL（含 GSV floor 和 DPB，但不需要 martingale test）
  3. NAV(i) = Assets BV(i) − BEL(i)，計算 NAV 的 P5/P50/P95 分布
  4. Duration Gap = Asset Duration − Liability Duration → 指導利率對沖決策

關鍵：ALM 用的是 P-measure 下的真實世界預期（股票均值 9%，長端利率比市場高 80bps）， 這才能評估在真實市場環境中公司的資本抗壓能力。 若改用 Q-measure（股票預期 3.5%），NAV 分布會嚴重低估公司資產的實際增值潛力， 導致對沖比例過重、SAA 股票倉位系統性偏低。

§8.A.0 Step 0: Model Selection Decision Tree 模型選擇決策樹

  ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ Q1. What is the purpose? → 直接決定 esg_config.yaml 的 mode 字段    │
  │   • Regulatory TVOG (Rule 19)        → mode: Q (風險中性)           │
  │   • IFRS 17 MRB measurement          → mode: Q (同上)               │
  │   • Internal ALM / Duration Gap      → mode: P (真實世界)           │
  │   • Capital planning / ORSA          → mode: P (真實世界)           │
  │                                                                      │
  │ Q2. What risk factors are MATERIAL to your liability portfolio?     │
  │   • Pure Non-Par Term/Endowment       → IR only is enough           │
  │   • UL with crediting floor          → IR + maybe credit            │
  │   • Par Fund (mixed asset)           → IR + Equity + Credit         │
  │   • VA / GMxB                        → IR + Equity + Vol surface    │
  │                                                                      │
  │ Q3. Complexity vs feasibility trade-off:                            │
  │   Quick prototype → HW1F + GBM Equity                               │
  │   Production HK   → HW2F (G2++) + Heston Equity + CIR Credit        │
  │   Industry top    → LMM + SLV Equity + Multi-factor credit          │
  │                                                                      │
  │ Recommended starting point for personal portfolio / new build:      │
  │   ⭐ HW1F (IR) + GBM (Equity, ρ-correlated with IR)                 │
  │   Reason: closed-form formulas for validation; 2 parameters each;   │
  │           sufficient for Par fund TVOG with reasonable accuracy.    │
  └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
對應 hkrbc-esg： 上述決策樹的選擇結果，直接翻譯為 config/esg_config.yaml 的頂層配置： 
hull_white_1f:
  a: 0.05       # Q2 選 HW1F → 填此區塊；若選 HW2F 則需自行擴展
  sigma: 0.010

equity:
  sigma_eq: 0.20  # Q2 選 Par Fund → 啟用此區塊（IR + Equity）

correlation:
  ir_eq: -0.20    # Q3 簡單起點 → 只填 ir_eq；其餘可先設為 0
Q1 的答案決定 simulation.n_scenarios（TVOG 用 ≥1,000；ALM 用 100–500）和
是否啟用 martingale_test（TVOG 必須開；ALM 可選）。

§8.A.1 Step 1: Yield Curve Bootstrapping 收益率曲線構建

香港行業核心原則：HKRBC 監管申報下，保險公司無需自行 bootstrap 收益率曲線， 而是直接使用 IA 按照 Cap. 41R Sch. 4 規定公佈的官方 RFR（Risk-Free Rate）曲線。 實際的 ESG 構建工作，是從這條官方曲線提取 forward rate 作為隨機模型的初始條件。

一、三個操作層次（香港行業實際分工）
層次應用場景操作方法工具
層次 1：監管申報 HKRBC 季度 EBS / TVOG 計算 直接讀入 IA 公佈的 Sch. 4 RFR 曲線（已含 LLP 前插值 + LLP 後 Smith-Wilson 外推），無需手動 bootstrap Prophet / AXIS 直接 plug-in；IA 官方數據文件
層次 2：ESG 初始條件 TVOG 隨機模擬（HW1F θ(t) 設定） 從 Sch. 4 zero rate 曲線 P(0,T) 用數值微分算出 instantaneous forward rate f(0,t)，作為 HW1F 漂移函數 θ(t) 的輸入 Python / Excel 數值微分
層次 3：內部 ALM / ORSA 內部壓力測試、新產品定價、ORSA（不受 Sch. 4 約束） IRS 報價 bootstrap（最常見）；或直接讀取 Bloomberg SWDF/IRDV 函數輸出 Bloomberg API；QuantLib BootstrapHelper
二、IA Sch. 4 曲線規格
貨幣Last Liquid Point (LLP)Convergence PointUFR G.N. 4006 of 2024LLP 以內數據源
HKD15 年60 年3.8%Bloomberg HKD IRS (HDSW1–HDSW15 BGN)
USD30 年60 年3.8%Bloomberg USD zero-coupon govt bond (I111xxY)
RMB10 年60 年4.6%ChinaBond Govt Bond Yield Curve (spot rate, 1–10Y)
GBP50 年80 年3.8%Bloomberg GBP IRS (BPSWS1–BPSWS50 BGN)
EUR20 年60 年3.8%Bloomberg EUR IRS (EUSA1–EUSA20 BGN)
SGD20 年60 年3.8%Bloomberg SGD zero-coupon govt bond (I107xxY)
JPY30 年60 年3.8%Bloomberg JPY zero-coupon govt bond (I018xxY)
TWD10 年60 年4.4%Bloomberg TWD zero-coupon govt bond (BI0594Z)
THB10 年60 年5.0%Bloomberg THB zero-coupon govt bond (BI0570Z)

來源：IA Gazette Notice G.N. 4006 of 2024（PDF） ，發布日期 2024年7月12日，生效日期 2024年7月1日。 完整宪报目錄見 IA Gazette Notices 頁面。

How Do Insurers Get Notified of UFR Updates in Practice?

In practice, insurers do not monitor the Gazette directly. The UFR reaches practitioners through the following channels (in order of frequency):

ChannelDescription
IA Circulars IA sends formal written circulars to licensed insurers ahead of any regulatory parameter change, flagging the relevant Gazette Notice
IA RBC Webpage The IA RBC dedicated page aggregates all related Gazette Notice links — no need to browse the full Gazette
ASHK / HKFI Bulletins The Actuarial Society of Hong Kong (ASHK) and Hong Kong Federation of Insurers (HKFI) circulate regulatory update summaries to members, including parameter changes
ESG / Vendor Data Feeds Providers such as Moody's Analytics and WTW package the latest IA RFR curves (UFR already embedded) into ready-to-import data files at each valuation date — most actuaries never need to look up the Gazette directly

Practical note: The UFR is not updated every quarter. G.N. 4006 was a one-off publication at HKRBC go-live (1 July 2024). A new Gazette Notice will only be issued if IA decides to revise the UFR. In day-to-day work, knowing "HKD UFR = 3.8% per G.N. 4006 of 2024" is sufficient.

Smith-Wilson 為何是香港標準：IA 採用 Smith-Wilson 方法（與歐洲 Solvency II 一致）對 LLP 以外的曲線進行外推， 核心優勢在於保證遠期曲線收斂至 UFR，避免長尾震盪，且參數具有完整可審計性。 Nelson-Siegel and other interpolation methods cannot guarantee convergence to UFR in the tail, hence not used for HKRBC regulatory reporting beyond LLP.

三、Smith-Wilson — How It Works (for intuition)

The core idea: the discount factor curve P(0, T) is decomposed into two parts — a long-run UFR anchor that takes over as T → ∞, plus a finite sum of "correction bumps" that pull the curve back towards observed market prices near and within the LLP.

P(0, T) = UFR anchor + market correction
\[ P(0,\,T) = \underbrace{e^{-\mathrm{UFR}\cdot T}}_{\text{UFR anchor}} \;+\; \underbrace{\sum_{i=1}^{N} \xi_i \cdot W(T,\,t_i)}_{\text{market correction}} \]
TermWhat It RepresentsIntuition
e−UFR · T The UFR anchor — discount factor if the world converged immediately to UFR from day 1 As T → ∞, the correction bumps decay to zero and P(0,T) → this term. This is how UFR convergence is enforced.
ξi Weights — one per market instrument (e.g. each IRS tenor within LLP) Solved by a linear system: "what weights make the model exactly reprice all N market instruments?"
W(T, ti) Kernel function — measures how strongly instrument i "influences" the curve at maturity T Influence decays with distance |T − ti|. Instruments far from T have little pull.
The Kernel Function W(T, u)
\[ W(T,\,u) = e^{-\mathrm{UFR}(T+u)} \Bigl[\,\alpha\cdot\min(T,u) - \tfrac{1}{2}e^{\,\alpha\min(T,u)} + \tfrac{1}{2}e^{-\alpha\min(T,u)}\Bigr] \]
ParameterMeaningWho Sets It
α Convergence speed — controls how fast the curve transitions from the market-fitted region to the UFR tail. Higher α = faster convergence. IA prescribes α (aligned with EIOPA's Solvency II standard)
min(T, u) Overlap between the projection maturity T and the instrument's maturity u — the kernel is symmetric Computed automatically
e−UFR(T+u) Decay envelope — ensures W → 0 as T → ∞, so the correction vanishes and UFR takes over Built into formula
Three-Step Build Process
  1. Collect inputs: N market IRS rates within LLP (e.g. HKD: 1Y, 2Y, …, 15Y), plus the UFR and α prescribed by IA.
  2. Solve for ξ: Set up an N×N linear system — "model price of each instrument = market price" — and solve for the N weights ξi. This is just matrix algebra: ξ = W_matrix⁻¹ · (P_market − P_UFR_anchor).
  3. Evaluate P(0, T) for any T: Plug T into the formula above. Within LLP, the result exactly fits market prices. Beyond LLP, the correction terms decay and UFR takes over smoothly.

Practical note: in day-to-day HKRBC work, IA publishes the final zero rates directly — you never need to run this calculation yourself. Understanding the formula matters mainly for ESG calibration discussions and model validation.

四、ESG 操作步驟：從 Sch. 4 零息曲線提取 Forward Rate

這是實際構建 ESG 時需要手動完成的部分（層次 2）： 

# 從 IA Sch. 4 公佈的 zero rate 數據，提取 HW1F 所需的 instantaneous forward rate
# 輸入：IA 公佈的 spot zero rate r(0, T)，單位：連續複利 (continuous compounding)

import numpy as np

# 步驟 1：從 IA 數據文件讀入 (T, r(0,T)) 對
tenors = np.array([0.5, 1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 10, 15, 20, 30, 50])      # 單位：年
zero_rates = np.array([0.040, 0.041, 0.042, 0.043, 0.045, ...])  # Sch. 4 公佈值

# 步驟 2：計算 discount factor P(0, T) = exp(-r(0,T) * T)
P = np.exp(-zero_rates * tenors)

# 步驟 3：用數值微分算 instantaneous forward rate
# f(0, t) = -d ln P(0,t) / dt ≈ -(ln P(0, t+dt) - ln P(0, t-dt)) / (2*dt)
dt = 1/12  # 月頻
t_grid = np.arange(dt, 101, dt)  # 投影至 100 年，月頻

# 對 ln P(0,t) 進行樣條插值（保單調性）
from scipy.interpolate import CubicSpline
lnP_interp = CubicSpline(tenors, np.log(P))
f0t = -lnP_interp(t_grid, 1)  # .derivative(1) = d/dt

# 步驟 4：輸出 f(0,t) 作為 HW1F theta(t) 計算的輸入（見 §8.A.3）
# f(0,t) 同時也是 ESG Martingale Test 的基準（§8.A.8）
五、Key Notes
  • IA publishes spot zero rates (continuously compounded) directly — not par yields. No further bootstrap needed; plug straight into discount factor P(0,T) = e−r·T.
  • The Sch. 4 curve already covers maturities beyond the LLP (Smith-Wilson tail included). No manual extrapolation required for regulatory reporting.
  • For ESG, the Sch. 4 curve at the valuation date is the t=0 anchor. The Martingale Test verifies that E[P(0,T)] across all scenarios equals the Sch. 4 market price.
  • For internal ALM stress tests or ORSA, companies may build their own curve from Bloomberg IRS quotes — not bound by Sch. 4 in that context.
  • "Bond bootstrap" (par yield → zero rate, classic fixed income concept) is different from "yield curve construction" in the ESG context. Since IA already provides zero rates, the main ESG task is interpolation and forward rate extraction — not bootstrapping from scratch.

§8.A.2 Step 2: Parameter Selection 參數選擇

For HW1F you need two parameters: a (mean reversion speed) and σ (short rate volatility). Two approaches:

Approach A: Calibrate to Swaption Vol Surface (proper way)
\[ \min_{a,\,\sigma} \sum_{k} \Bigl[\, \mathrm{IV}^{\mathrm{model}}(K_k, T_k;\, a, \sigma) \;-\; \mathrm{IV}^{\mathrm{market}}(K_k, T_k) \,\Bigr]^{2} \]
IV = swaption implied volatility  |  \(K_k\) = strike  |  \(T_k\) = expiry  |  \(a\) = mean reversion speed  |  \(\sigma\) = short rate vol
Convention: Bachelier (normal) vol for near-zero / negative rate environments; Black-76 (lognormal) for positive rate regimes

Use closed-form HW1F swaption pricing (Jamshidian's trick decomposes a swaption into a portfolio of options on zero-coupon bonds). Solve via scipy.optimize.least_squares.

Approach B: Pick Plausible Defaults (quick start)
ParameterTypical RangeIndustry Default for HKDSensitivity
a (mean reversion)0.01 - 0.10~ 0.05Higher a → faster convergence to long-run mean → less long-end vol
σ (short rate vol)0.005 - 0.015 (50-150 bps)~ 0.010 (100 bps)Higher σ → more dispersion → larger TVOG
ρ (IR-Equity correlation)−0.3 to +0.3~ +0.2Higher ρ → equity rises when rates rise
σS (equity vol)0.15 - 0.30~ 0.18 (18%)Higher σS → larger equity-driven TVOG

§8.A.3 Step 3: Drift Function θ(t) — Curve-Fitting Trick 漂移函數推導

Hull-White's key insight: by choosing θ(t) appropriately, the model exactly reproduces the initial yield curve. This is why HW1F is so popular for ESG. The required θ(t):

\[ \theta(t) = \frac{\partial f(0,t)}{\partial t} + a\cdot f(0,t) + \frac{\sigma^2}{2a}\!\left(1 - e^{-2at}\right) \]

where f(0, t) = instantaneous forward rate at time 0 for maturity t (from Step 1's curve).

Practical computation:

  • Use numerical differentiation: ∂f/∂t ≈ (f(t+Δt) − f(t))/Δt with Δt = 0.01 yr
  • Pre-compute θ(t) at all simulation grid points before MC; store as array
  • Alternative formulation: use α(t) = f(0,t) + (σ²/2a²)(1 − e−at)2; then r(t) = x(t) + α(t) where x(t) is a mean-zero OU process. This avoids θ entirely and is numerically more stable.

§8.A.4 Step 4: Discretization 連續模型離散化

HW1F has an exact discretization (rare luxury — most models need Euler-Maruyama approximation):

\[ r(t+\Delta t) = r(t)\,e^{-a\Delta t} + M(t,\,t{+}\Delta t) + \sqrt{V(\Delta t)}\cdot Z \] \[ M(t,\,t{+}\Delta t) = \alpha(t{+}\Delta t) - \alpha(t)\,e^{-a\Delta t} \qquad\text{(conditional mean)} \] \[ V(\Delta t) = \frac{\sigma^2}{2a}\!\left(1 - e^{-2a\Delta t}\right) \qquad\text{(conditional variance)},\quad Z\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1) \]

Time step choice:

ΔtProsCons
Monthly (1/12)Industry standard; balances accuracy & speedLoses intra-month dynamics
Quarterly (1/4)Faster; aligns with reporting cyclePath-dependent payoffs less accurate
Daily (1/252)Most accurate50x slower than monthly

§8.A.5 Step 5: Monte Carlo Setup 蒙特卡羅模擬設置

Design ChoiceRecommendationRationale
Scenario count N 1,000 minimum; 5,000 standard; 10,000+ for tail SE ∝ 1/√N — diminishing returns past 10K
Antithetic Variates Always ON Free 50% variance reduction; just negate Brownian increments for pair
Quasi-MC (Sobol) Use for high-dim problems Convergence √N → N; available via scipy.stats.qmc.Sobol
Random seed Fix the seed for reproducibility Audit / regression requires bit-exact replay
Projection horizon 100 years (or until last policy maturity + 10 yrs) Tail policies (whole life, annuities) need long horizon
Path storage Save as compressed Parquet / HDF5; not Excel 5000 × 1200 monthly = 6M data points per risk factor; Excel will crash

§8.A.6 Step 6: Multi-Factor Correlation via Cholesky 多因子相關性

For 2 factors (IR, Equity) with correlation ρ:

\[ \Sigma = \begin{pmatrix} 1 & \rho \\ \rho & 1 \end{pmatrix},\qquad L = \operatorname{chol}(\Sigma) = \begin{pmatrix} 1 & 0 \\ \rho & \sqrt{1-\rho^2} \end{pmatrix} \] \[ \mathbf{Z}_{\text{correlated}} = L\,\mathbf{Z}_{\text{independent}} \]

For k factors, build the k×k correlation matrix Σ, take Cholesky factor L (numpy: np.linalg.cholesky(Sigma)), multiply each timestep's independent N(0,1) vector by L to get correlated shocks.

Practical correlation matrix for HK Par fund (illustrative):

IREquityCredit SpreadFX (USD)
IR1.0+0.2−0.3+0.1
Equity+0.21.0−0.4+0.05
Credit Spread−0.3−0.41.0+0.05
FX (USD)+0.1+0.05+0.051.0

Source: historical correlation over 5-10 yrs; should be reviewed annually. In practice, correlations need to be positive semi-definite; if not, project to nearest PSD matrix (e.g. via eigenvalue truncation).

§8.A.7 Step 7: Validation Battery — 5 Tests You Must Pass 必過的5個驗證測試

#TestMathematical StatementHow to ComputePass Criterion
1 Martingale ZCB Test E[exp(−∫₀ᵀ rs ds)] = P(0, T) for all T For each T, compute integral via trapezoidal rule on rate path; take exp; average across scenarios; compare to market P(0,T) Error < 5 bps at all key tenors (1, 5, 10, 20, 30y)
2 Martingale Equity Test E[ST / BT] = S0 for all T BT = exp(∫₀ᵀ rs ds); compute ST/BT per scenario; average Within 2 SE of S0 for each T
3 Market Price Re-pricing Calibration instruments (swaptions, equity options) re-priced by MC ≈ market Price each calibration option via MC payoff averaging; compare to market quote Within 5% of market price; ideally within bid-ask spread
4 Moment Test Empirical moments (mean, var, skew, kurt) of generated paths match theoretical For each t, compute moments across scenarios; compare to closed-form (HW1F has analytical moments) Within 2 SE
5 Distribution Test (Q-Q Plot) Log-returns approximately normal; tails not too fat/thin Q-Q plot of log-returns vs normal; visual inspection + KS test No systematic deviation; KS p-value > 0.05

§8.A.8 Step 8: Output Standardization 輸出標準化

ESG output must be in a standardized format consumable by downstream models (Prophet, AXIS, internal Python TVOG engines).

(a) Recommended Scenario File Schema
ColumnTypeExampleNotes
scenario_idint1 to 5000Identify each scenario

currencystrHKD / USD / RMBFor multi-currency
rfr_shortfloat0.0412Short rate rt
